TL;DR: 本文研究了II型自发参量下转换（SPDC）产生的双光子波函数中空间与偏振自由度的耦合关系，通过识别偏振与实空间坐标之间的空间关联方向，设计了沿该关联方向限制收集的孔径形状。数值模拟和实验均表明，这种关联对齐孔径能同时提高纠缠纯度和光子收集效率，为高性能纠缠光子源提供了新的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: II型SPDC是产生偏振纠缠光子对最常用的方法之一，但其双光子波函数因双折射效应在空间与偏振自由度之间存在复杂耦合，限制了纠缠纯度和亮度。理解并利用这一耦合关系来优化光源设计，是实现高纯度、高亮度纠缠光子源的关键。

Method: 从II型SPDC在BBO晶体中产生的双光子波函数出发，计算双光子偏振态的实空间分布；通过识别偏振与实空间坐标之间的空间关联方向，设计特定形状的孔径沿着该关联方向进行限制性收集；采用数值模拟与实验相结合的方式验证设计效果。

Result: 数值模拟和实验均证明，采用关联对齐的孔径形状可以同时实现更高的纠缠纯度和更高的光子收集效率，优于传统孔径设计方案。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了基于实空间双光子波函数优化孔径几何形状的实用设计原则，为开发高纯度、高亮度的SPDC纠缠光子源提供了新途径。

Abstract: Spontaneous parametric down-conversion (SPDC) is one of the most widely used sources of polarization-entangled photon pairs, and understanding the generated biphoton state is essential for realizing high-brightness and high-purity entangled-photon sources. In particular, Type-II SPDC produces a biphoton wavefunction with a complex coupling between the spatial and polarization degrees of freedom owing to birefringence. In this study, we calculate the real-space distribution of the two-photon polarization state from the biphoton wavefunction of Type-II SPDC generated in a \b{eta}-barium borate (BBO) crystal. By identifying the spatial correlation direction between polarization and real-space coordinates, we designed aperture shapes that restrict the collection along this correlation direction. Through both numerical simulations and experiments, we demonstrate that such correlation-aligned apertures simultaneously achieve higher entanglement purity and improved photon collection efficiency. These results establish a practical design principle for optimizing aperture geometries based on the real-space biphoton wavefunction, providing a new approach to realizing high-purity and high-brightness SPDC entangled-photon sources.

</details>


### [100] [On supporting affine functionals for Entanglement of Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27363)
*A. S. Holevo,M. E. Shirokov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在量子信息文献中，通常假设纠缠形成熵（EoF）的凸屋顶结构和子系统有限维性质保证了任意量子态上EoF存在全局支撑仿射泛函，但作者通过一个显式反例（两个量子比特系统）证明当态简并时这一假设不成立。论文还给出了有限维和无限维双粒子量子系统中局部和全局支撑仿射泛函存在的条件，并利用这些条件推导出有限秩态上EoF的Lipschitz下半连续界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正量子信息领域中一个被广泛接受但实际错误的假设：即EoF的凸屋顶结构和有限维子系统总能保证任意态上存在全局支撑仿射泛函。作者通过具体反例揭示了这一假设的局限性，并系统性地描述了支撑仿射泛函存在的确切条件，为后续研究提供正确的基础。

Method: 作者首先利用已有等价性结果：EoF在某态ρ处存在支撑仿射泛函等价于该EoF在ρ处的Lipschitz下半连续性。然后借助Wootters公式（用于计算两量子比特系统的EoF）以及AI工具Claude Fable 5的帮助，构造了一个两量子比特系统中简并态ρ，使得该Lipschitz下半连续性不成立，从而构成反例。随后，论文进一步推导了有限维和无限维双粒子系统中局部与全局支撑仿射泛函存在的充要条件，并据此给出有限秩态上EoF的Lipschitz下半连续性界。

Result: 作者构造了A和B均为量子比特系统时的一个显式简并态反例，证明在该态上EoF的全局支撑仿射泛函不存在，从而否定了先前文献中的普遍假设。此外，论文推导了局部和全局支撑仿射泛函存在的条件，并得到了有限秩态ρ处EoF的Lipschitz下半连续界（形式为E_F(ρ)-E_F(σ)≤C_ρ‖ρ-σ‖₁），同时考虑了是否对σ的支持（支撑集）施加限制的两种情形。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一个重要的技术性修正：简单假设凸屋顶结构和有限维度并不足以保证EoF在所有态上存在支撑仿射泛函，特别是对简并态而言。论文提供的条件性框架和所得Lipschitz界为后续在量子纠缠理论中正确应用EoF的性质提供了更严谨的数学依据。

Abstract: In several articles, the authors assume that the convex roof structure of the EoF and finite-dimensionality of subsystems $A$ and $B$ guarantee the existence of the (global) supporting affine functional for the EoF at any state of the system $AB$. This means that for any state $ρ$ of $AB$ there is a Hermitian operator $Λ_ρ$ on $\mathcal{H}_{AB}=\mathcal{H}_A\otimes\mathcal{H}_B$ such that $E_F(ρ)=\mathrm{Tr}Λ_ρρ$ and $E_F(σ)\geq\mathrm{Tr}Λ_ρσ$ for any state $σ$ of $AB$. We present an explicit example showing that, when $ρ$ is degenerate, this is not true even in the simplest case when $A$ and $B$ are qubit systems. The construction is based on the fact that the existence of a supporting affine functional for the EoF at a state $ρ$ is equivalent to the Lipschitz lower semicontinuity of the EoF at this state $ρ$. We use Wootters' formula and the help of Claude Fable 5 to find a state $ρ$ of the system $AB$ for which the latter property does not hold.
  We also describe conditions for the existence the local and global supporting affine functionals for the EoF at a given state of both finite and infinite-dimensional bipartite quantum systems. These conditions allow us to find Lipschitz lower semicontinuity bounds for the EoF at a given finite rank state $ρ$ (i.e. inequalities of the form $\,E_F(ρ)-E_F(σ)\leq C_ρ\|ρ-σ\|_1$) with and without restrictions on the support of the state $σ$.

</details>


### [101] [Spectral Fingerprints of Gauge Theories on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27457)
*Graham Van Goffrier,Debasish Banerjee,Bipasha Chakraborty,Emilie Huffman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种聚焦式最大混合态谱采样算法，可针对哈密顿量谱的任意区域（从基态临界现象到中间谱热化行为）进行有限分辨率谱分布提取，并在(1+1)d非阿贝尔SO(3)规范理论上进行了演示，使用78个双量子比特门深度的电路在超导量子硬件上实现了近期的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最大混合态谱采样算法虽然能提取哈密顿量的整体谱分布，但缺乏对谱中特定区域的聚焦能力，难以针对性地研究基态量子临界性（低能区）或热化行为（中间谱区）等不同物理现象。此外，近端量子硬件上运行该算法所需资源（量子比特数、电路深度）和编译策略也需要进一步优化。

Method: 作者展示了如何将最大混合态谱采样算法聚焦于谱中任意感兴趣的区域，以学习模型的完整"指纹"。具体方法包括：(1) 在(1+1)d非阿贝尔SO(3)规范理论上进行算法演示；(2) 利用新兴的动态电路能力将所需量子比特数约减一半；(3) 提出一种基于Pauli-frame优化的受控时间演化编译新策略；(4) 利用该方法的量子读出误差缓解的天然优势。

Result: 使用深度为78个双量子比特门的电路在超导量子硬件上成功执行了该算法。演示了两个物理应用：无序多体转变和中间谱态密度，并说明了这些应用所需的除算法傅里叶输出之外的后处理步骤。动态电路方法将所需量子比特数大致减半，且量子读出误差缓解是该方法的简易附加优势。

Conclusion: 最大混合态谱采样算法通过聚焦策略可以高效地研究哈密顿量谱中任意区域的物理现象，结合动态电路和Pauli-frame编译优化，该算法在近期超导量子硬件上是可行的，为量子临界性、热化和无序转变等物理研究提供了实用的量子计算工具。

Abstract: Maximally mixed state spectral sampling is an unbiased quantum algorithm that allows for extraction of a finite-resolution spectral distribution from a Hamiltonian over potentially the entire allowed range of energies. We show how it may be focused on any desired area of the spectrum in order to learn about the full \textit{fingerprint} of the model of interest: from its ground state phenomena such as quantum criticality, obtained from the lowest lying energies, to its thermalization behavior, obtained from the mid-spectrum. We demonstrate this technique specifically on a $(1+1)d$ non-Abelian $SO(3)$ gauge theory, providing a comprehensive analysis of the steps necessary for performing this algorithm, as well as what is possible in the near-term with superconducting quantum hardware, performing simulations with circuits that are $78$ two-qubit gates deep. We show how this algorithm is able to take advantage of emerging dynamical circuit capabilities in near-term hardware to roughly halve the number required qubits, as well as how quantum readout error mitigation is trivial for this method. Along the way, we propose a novel strategy for compiling the controlled-time evolutions needed for spectral sampling by means of Pauli-frame optimizations. We illustrate two physical applications of quantum spectral sampling -- disordered many-body transitions, and mid-spectrum densities of states -- and what postprocessing steps they require beyond the Fourier outputs of the algorithm.

</details>


### [102] [Auditing Structured Randomness for Quantum Error Correction under a Bounded Cloud Fault Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26600)
*Ziqing Guo,Anthony Lawrence,Renyu Wang,Randy Kuang,Ziwen Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对云量子处理器中量子纠错电路的编译安全问题，提出通过每次运行重新播种（per-run reseeding）编码器来抵御故障注入攻击。文章定义了"可接受逻辑扰动"度量，用于衡量被接受结果中有害逻辑作用的权重期望值，并通过密集线性代数和门级稳定子仿真对比了攻击者在编码器已知前/后选择故障两种场景，结果显示重新播种可将平均可接受逻辑扰动从0.150降至0.020（降低86.7%），该降低主要来源于拒绝机制而非精确纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云量子处理器编译量子纠错电路时可能与不可信的工作负载共存。固定的公开编码器为故障注入攻击者提供了可重复利用的目标，而精确的Haar随机编码器电路成本呈指数级增长。现有的高效随机编码器集成只提供平均矩保证，未刻画最坏情况下的可接受损坏，因此需要一种系统化的方法来量化和缓解此类安全风险。

Method: 本文定义了"可接受逻辑扰动"（accepted logical disturbance），一种基于接受加权（acceptance-weighted）的度量，用于衡量被接受结果中有害逻辑作用的期望值，并推导了其精确的Haar期望值。随后评估了一个多项式成本的加扰（seeded）Clifford编码器家族，采用密集线性代数和门级稳定子仿真，分别针对在编码器已知之前或之后选择故障的攻击者场景进行测试。

Result: 重新播种将平均可接受逻辑扰动从0.150（针对学习每个编码器后选择的故障）降至0.020（针对编码器已知前选择的一个故障），降幅达86.7%。该降低主要由拒绝机制（rejection）导致。固定距离三的[[5,1,3]]码能纠正所有测试的重量一泡利错误，而所选集成中仅18.5%的采样编码器满足精确量子纠错条件。

Conclusion: 测量结果量化了重新播种带来的完整性增益，并在明确的故障模型和攻击者知识模型下，将后选检测（postselected detection）与精确纠错（exact correction）分离。研究为云量子处理器在不可信环境下编译量子纠错电路提供了安全性的定量评估框架。

Abstract: Cloud quantum processors compile submitted quantum error correction circuits and may colocate them with untrusted workloads. A fixed public encoder gives a fault-injection adversary a reusable target. Per-run reseeding changes the physical-to-logical fault map. Exact Haar-random encoders have exponential circuit cost. Efficient random ensembles provide average-moment guarantees and leave worst-case accepted corruption uncharacterized. We define accepted logical disturbance, an acceptance-weighted measure of harmful logical action in accepted results, and derive its exact Haar expectation. We evaluate a polynomial-cost seeded Clifford encoder family using dense linear algebra and gate-level stabilizer simulation against faults chosen before or after the encoder is known. Reseeding reduces mean accepted logical disturbance from 0.150 for faults chosen after learning each encoder to 0.020 for one fault chosen before it is known. The 86.7% reduction results from rejection. The fixed distance-three \([[5,1,3]]\) code corrects every tested weight-one Pauli, while 18.5% of sampled encoders in the selected ensemble satisfy exact quantum error correction. The measured reduction quantifies the integrity gain from reseeding and separates postselected detection from exact correction under explicit fault and attacker-knowledge models.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum-enhanced single and multiparameter metrology in qutrit ensembles by generalized twisting dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26630)
*Deep Datta,Sayam Chakraborty,John Drew Wilson,Vaibhav Madhok,Athreya Shankar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子三能级系统（qutrit）集合在量子增强传感中的应用，提出了三种实验中可行的基于单轴扭转（OAT）模型的qutrit泛化方案，并利用量子Fisher信息工具表征其计量学性能，发现三者均可在多达6个编码方向上实现近乎海森堡极限的标度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子集合的量子增强传感此前主要聚焦于基于量子比特（qubit）的协议，但许多实验平台已具备相干控制和纠缠多能级系统的能力。本文旨在探索利用qutrit（三能级）集合进行量子增强传感的潜力，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出三种实验中可行的OAT模型qutrit推广方案（纠缠操作仅涉及两个能级，第三个能级作为旁观者）；利用量子Fisher信息工具箱表征动态生成态的计量学效用；通过有效时间反转协议讨论实验可行性；研究估计歧义和局部耗散等实际问题并通过优化传感器工作点加以解决；分析多参数同时估计时的测量不相容性问题。

Result: 数值结果表明三种模型在编码方向上提供了相当大的自由度，在短演化时间后多达6个方向（共8个）展现出近乎海森堡极限的标度精度；优化工作点可克服估计歧义和局部耗散；多参数同时估计的测量不相容性在零工作点随系统尺寸增大而被抑制；其中一个模型实现了具有一对对易生成元的近乎海森堡极限计量，这是SU(2)量子比特传感器所不具备的。

Conclusion: qutrit集合为量子增强传感提供了比qubit系统更丰富的可能性，尤其是基于su(3)代数可实现具有对易生成元的近海森堡极限计量，这在qubit集合的集体SU(2)旋转传感中是不可能实现的。

Abstract: Quantum-enhanced sensing with atomic ensembles has predominantly focused on qubit-based protocols, despite the growing ability of many experimental platforms to coherently control and entangle multi-level systems. Here, we investigate quantum-enhanced sensing with qutrit ensembles by introducing three experimentally feasible qutrit generalisations of the one-axis twisting (OAT) model that involve entangling operations only between two levels, while the third level primarily acts as a spectator. We characterize the metrological utility of the dynamically generated states using the quantum Fisher information toolbox. We find numerically that all three models offer considerable freedom in encoding direction for quantum-enhanced sensing, with up to 6 out of 8 possible directions exhibit near-Heisenberg scaling after a short evolution time. We discuss experimental access to this enhanced metrological precision via effective time-reversal protocols. Furthermore, we examine the practical issues of estimation ambiguity and local dissipation, and show that they can be largely overcome by optimizing the sensor operating point. Finally, we show that the measurement incompatibility in estimating multiple parameters simultaneously encoded in different directions with near-Heisenberg scaling of precision is suppressed at the zero operating point as the system size increases. In the process, we find that one of the models enables near-Heisenberg scaling metrology with a pair of commuting generators, a possibility that arises from the $su(3)$ algebra and is thus absent in qubit-ensemble based sensors of collective $SU(2)$ rotations.

</details>


### [104] [Quantum estimation theory with quantum noise control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26635)
*Osamu Hirota,Masaki Sohma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于量子估计理论，扩展了Monras和Paris在估计界实现问题上的开创性工作，利用约半个世纪前提出的广义外差探测概念，提出一种能满足下一代量子信息机制对量子优势和实时处理双重需求的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代量子信息机制迫切需要同时满足两个严苛要求：一是提供经典理论无法实现的量子优势保证，二是满足无延迟的严格实时处理需求。现有基于Monras和Paris的估计界实现方案尚未充分面向这些实际需求进行扩展，因此需要探索其实用化方向。

Method: 通过引入约五十年前提出的广义外差探测（generalized heterodyne detection）概念，对Monras和Paris在量子估计界实现方面的原始思想进行实用化扩展，构建满足近未来信息技术实时性和量子优势要求的方案。

Result: 研究表明，基于Monras和Paris结果的方案在结合广义外差探测后，能够有效满足近未来信息技术的严苛要求，即同时实现量子优势保障与无延迟实时处理。

Conclusion: 本文展示了Monras和Paris的量子估计理论思想具有切实的实用扩展方向，通过广义外差探测可使其满足下一代量子信息技术的实时性和量子优势需求，为该领域应用研究提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum estimation theory serves as one of fundamental backbones of quantum Shannon information theory and was mathematically systematized by pioneers of quantum information science. Although its applied research saw limited progress for a long period, the 21st century has witnessed active discussions and numerous fruitful proposals regarding its applications. Notably, the contributions of Monras and Paris represent one of the most remarkable achievements addressing the realization problem of estimation bounds. This paper aims to demonstrate a practical direction for extending their original ideas. Next-generation quantum information mechanisms urgently demand guarantees of quantum advantage unattainable by classical theory, alongside strict real-time processing without delays. By leveraging the concept of generalized heterodyne detection proposed nearly half a century ago, we demonstrate that a scheme based on their results can effectively meet the stringent requirements of near-future information technologies.

</details>


### [105] [A thermometric quantum Brownian model for low-temperature electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26646)
*Harry T. H. Fung,Thomas M. Stace*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子布朗运动方程（QBME）的电阻器热力学模型，用于在低温下模拟耗散电子学，并与传统的量子光学主方程（QOME）在transmon与电阻并联的实例中进行比较，发现两者预测相近但物理上有区别，并提出实验判别方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电子学中电阻器模型通常基于QOME，但QOME忽略了多个基本物理性质，限制了其模拟某些超导现象的能力，因此需要一种能更准确模拟低温耗散电子学的替代模型。

Method: 从量子布朗运动方程（QBME）出发，提出一种电阻器的热力学模型（thermometric QBME），使其能够实际应用于低温耗散电子学的建模；以transmon并联电阻为简单示例，将其预测与QOME进行对比分析。

Result: 热力学QBME与QOME在transmon并联电阻的示例中产生了可比拟但物理上截然不同的预测结果；作者进一步提出了可用于判别这两种模型适用性的潜在实验测试方案。

Conclusion: 热力学QBME为低温耗散电子学提供了可行的建模工具，与QOME相比更能反映电阻器的基本物理性质；通过实验测试可判别两种模型在实际实验中的适用性。

Abstract: Quantum mechanical models of resistors in quantum electronics are often based on the quantum optical master equation (QOME). The QOME overlooks several fundamental properties, limiting its ability to model certain superconducting phenomena. Here we present a thermometric model of resistors, adapted from the quantum Brownian motion equation (QBME), that facilitates practical use of the QBME in modelling dissipative electronics at low temperatures. We compare the thermometric QBME presented here with predictions of the QOME, in the simple example of a transmon shunted by a resistor. We show that both the QBME and QOME yield comparable but physically distinct predictions, and discuss potential experimental tests with which to discriminate their use in modelling practical experiments.

</details>


### [106] [Observing relativistic trajectories of single photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26778)
*Sayantan Das,Daniel S. Dahl,Daniel Peace,Marcelo P. Almeida,Abhishek Roy,Markus Rambach,Andrew G. White,Timothy C. Ralph,Jacquiline Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过实验在Michelson-Sagnac干涉仪中重构了单光子的相对论Bohmian轨迹，观测到了光子在干涉条纹中穿越时的亚光速和超光速特征，为相对论Bohmian力学的实验研究提供了途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子力学诠释不为粒子赋予确定轨迹，而Bohmian诠释虽然做到了这一点，但直到最近才提出了将其与相对论协调的算符方法。作者旨在通过实验验证和探索这一新理论的物理预测。

Method: 在Michelson-Sagnac干涉仪中让反向传播的概率幅以光速面对面干涉，通过实验手段重构单个光子的相对论Bohmian轨迹。

Result: 实验观测到光子的Bohmian轨迹在穿越干涉条纹时表现出亚光速和超光速特征，与相对论Bohmian理论的预测一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为相对论Bohmian力学提供了实验可及性，使得探索其非直观和反直觉的性质成为可能。

Abstract: While the standard interpretation of quantum mechanics does not assign definite trajectories to particles, the Bohmian interpretation does. Only recently has an operational method for reconciling Bohmian mechanics with relativity been proposed. Here, we experimentally reconstruct relativistic Bohmian trajectories of a single photon in a Michelson-Sagnac interferometer, where counter-propagating probability amplitudes interfere head-on at the speed of light. As predicted by the relativistic Bohmian theory, we observe subluminal and superluminal features of the Bohmian trajectories of the photon traversing through the fringes. Our work provides experimental access to relativistic Bohmian mechanics and enables exploration of its unusual and counterintuitive properties.

</details>


### [107] [Uncertainty limits for post-selected metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26781)
*Ming Ji,Holger F. Hofmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了后选择计量学中量子Fisher信息（QFI）定义的条件生成元不确定性与Ozawa-Hall不确定性之间的对应关系，并解释了后选择为何能超越生成元本征值的最大不确定性从而实现极端测量灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解后选择计量学中异常灵敏度增强的物理机制，特别是条件QFI与量子测量理论中不确定性概念之间的深层联系。

Method: 将后选择QFI给出的条件生成元不确定性映射到Ozawa-Hall不确定性的理论框架中，分析后选择测量结果如何根据该结果的量子统计更新生成元的不确定性，并研究QFI超越生成元本征值最大不确定性的条件。

Result: 证明后选择QFI的条件生成元不确定性即为Ozawa-Hall不确定性；后选择将初始生成元不确定性的大部分集中于低概率的后选择测量结果中，从而产生异常条件不确定性，实现超越极限的计量灵敏度。

Conclusion: 异常条件不确定性是后选择计量学中极端灵敏度增强的根本原因，该机制可通过Ozawa-Hall不确定性框架统一理解。

Abstract: For unitary transformations, the quantum Fisher information (QFI) of a pure state is given by the uncertainty of the generator in that state. In post-selected metrology, the QFI is given by a modified expression describing conditional quantum statistics of the generator. Here, we show that the conditional generator uncertainties defined by post-selected QFI correspond to Ozawa-Hall uncertainties known from the theoretical analysis of quantum measurements. The post-selected measurement outcome updates the generator uncertainty according to the quantum statistics of that outcome. Enhancements of QFI beyond the maximal uncertainties of the generator eigenvalues are possible because post-selection tends to concentrate the largest part of the initial generator uncertainty in low probability outcomes of the post-selection measurement. Anomalous conditional uncertainties thus explain the extreme sensitivities that can be achieved in post-selected metrology.

</details>


### [108] [Corrections induced by the GUP to the Lamb shift of an accelerated atom interacting with a quantum scalar field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26914)
*Zhi Wang,Yi Yang,Zhengwen Long*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在DDC形式主义框架下，研究广义不确定性原理（GUP）对两能级原子与无质量标量量子场相互作用时Lamb位移的修正效应，涉及惯性运动、匀加速运动和匀速圆周运动三种轨迹下的真空涨落与辐射反作用贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GUP作为量子引力候选效应在原子物理中的可观测后果，特别是其对Lamb位移的修正，评估不同运动模式（惯性、匀加速、匀速圆周）下GUP修正的差异，为量子引力理论的实验检验提供理论依据。

Method: 采用Dalibard-Dupont-Roc-Cohen-Tannoudji（DDC）形式主义，分别计算真空涨落和辐射反作用对辐射能移的贡献。首先推导三种运动轨迹下场统计函数的频率积分表达式，然后将其代入计算两能级原子的辐射能移，并分离出GUP引入的β比例修正项。

Result: GUP修正完全来自真空涨落，且与β成正比。匀加速情形下修正包含热和非热两部分：低加速度时热部分呈非单调行为且正比于a^4（a/ω0→0极限），非热部分非线性单调增长，在加速度大时比热部分大近两个数量级。匀速圆周运动下修正纯属非热效应，与加速度呈非线性单调依赖，根据β符号陡升或陡降。相同β下，圆周运动的修正大于匀加速运动，因为前者包含a^2和a^3项而后者仅含a^2项。

Conclusion: GUP对Lamb位移的修正仅起源于真空涨落，加速度相关的GUP修正在不同运动模式（匀加速与匀速圆周）下表现出显著不同的标度行为（a^4 vs a^2/a^3），可为零量子引力修正的探测提供理论指引。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of the GUP on the Lamb shift of a two-level atom interacting with a real massless scalar quantum field, within the DDC formalism. For an atom undergoing inertial motion, uniform acceleration, and uniform circular motion, we analyze the separate contributions of vacuum fluctuations and radiation reaction. We first derive the statistical functions of the field along the atom's trajectories for the three types of motion, expressing them as frequency integrals, and then employ them to calculate the vacuum fluctuation and radiation reaction contributions to the radiative level shift. We show that the GUP-modified Lamb shift of the two-level atom arises entirely from vacuum fluctuations and acquires additional corrections proportional to $β$. We focus in particular on the acceleration-dependent GUP corrections. For a uniformly accelerated atom, the GUP corrections comprise thermal and nonthermal parts. At low accelerations, the thermal part exhibits nonmonotonic behavior, and is proportional to $a^4$ in the limit $a/ω_0 \to 0$; the nonthermal part, by contrast, grows nonlinearly and monotonically, exceeding the thermal part by nearly two orders of magnitude at large accelerations. For an atom in uniform circular motion, the GUP corrections are purely nonthermal and also display a nonlinear, monotonic dependence on acceleration, increasing or decreasing steeply according to the sign of $β$. For the same $β$, the corrections are larger in uniform circular motion than in uniformly accelerated motion, since the former involves terms proportional to both $a^2$ and $a^3$, whereas the latter contains only $a^2$ terms.

</details>


### [109] [Quantum Rare-Event Estimation for Ising Graphical Models with Belief-Propagation State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26840)
*Mario Hernández Vera,Narges Jamialahmadi,Burak Mete,Susanne Schneider,Marco De Pascale*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种近似策略来降低量子振幅估计在相关Ising图模型稀有事件概率估计中的实现难度：一是结合loopy belief propagation与Chow–Liu算法的无采样态制备方法（线性门数和深度），二是用可逆布尔门构建结构化oracle来评估阈值规则。在二十节点供应链中断案例中，量子估计器在相同渐近误差标度下比四种经典蒙特卡洛基线获得更低的常数因子估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子振幅估计有望降低稀有事件概率估计的采样成本，但将其应用于相关Ising图模型时面临两大障碍：目标分布难以制备，以及缺乏实际可行的事件oracle。本文旨在探索近似策略来缓解这些挑战。

Method: 提出两种近似策略：(1) 无采样态制备方法，将loopy belief propagation与Chow–Liu算法结合，生成树近似分布并编译为线性门数和深度的量子电路；(2) 利用可逆布尔门构建结构化的oracle来评估阈值规则。在二十节点供应链中断模型上，将最大似然振幅估计与四种经典蒙特卡洛基线比较。

Result: 在固定深度调度下，量子估计器与经典方法具有相同的渐近误差标度，但以常数因子降低估计误差。当以振幅编码查询而非原始采样次数作为资源度量时，这一优势缩小。文章还区分了统计误差与近似态制备及oracle构造导致的确定性误差，并识别了实现超越常数因子改进所需的条件。

Conclusion: 量子振幅估计在近似态制备和结构化oracle下可获得常数因子级的精度优势，但要实现超越常数因子的改进，需要更好的态制备精度和oracle设计，这为未来工作指明了方向。

Abstract: Quantum amplitude estimation can reduce the sampling cost of rare-event probability estimation, but applying it to correlated Ising graphical models is limited by the difficulty of preparing the target distribution and building a practical event oracle. This work explores two approximate strategies for mitigating these challenges. We introduce a sample-free state-preparation method combining loopy belief propagation with the Chow--Liu algorithm. The resulting tree approximation is compiled into a quantum circuit with linear gate count and depth, and its accuracy is evaluated across graph families spanning different topologies, coupling strengths, and coupling signs. We also construct a structural oracle that evaluates threshold rules with reversible Boolean gates. Using a twenty-node supply-chain disruption model as a case study, we compare maximum likelihood amplitude estimation against four classical Monte Carlo baselines. Under the fixed-depth schedule used throughout this work, the quantum estimator has the same asymptotic error scaling as the classical methods but achieves lower estimation error by a constant factor. This reduction narrows when amplitude-encoding queries replace raw shots as the resource metric. We separate statistical error from the deterministic errors caused by approximate state preparation and oracle construction, and identify the requirements for achieving an improvement beyond a constant factor.

</details>


### [110] [Energy Ordering from Nonlinear Quantum Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26845)
*Alireza Ataei,Olle Eriksson,Vahid Azimi-Mousolou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了能量有序占据（类似构造原理/奥夫鲍原理）可以自发地从非线性量子耗散动力学中涌现，而非依赖外部热库或人为的占据原则。具体而言，量子Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert动力学使得任意一般性初始混合态内在演化形成能量层级排序，该收敛源于Lyapunov单调性和无序占据构型的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量有序占据（从原子中电子填充的构造原理到量子多体系统的低能构型涌现）是量子物理中的深层规律，但其动力学机制一直是一个基本未解问题——即一个一般性量子态如何动态地发展出这种能量层次结构。

Method: 作者提出能量层级并非通过原则性假设或热库耦合产生，而是在量子Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert（LLG）动力学下从一般初始混合态内在涌现。这种收敛是Lyapunov单调性与无序布居构型不稳定性的非平凡结果，并通过数值模拟验证了分析预测。

Result: 数值模拟确认了分析预测，表明系统确实收敛到低能扇区，验证了非线性量子耗散可作为组织密度矩阵布居的内在机制，并为选择性制备低能子空间提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了通过非线性量子耗散实现能量有序占据的内在动力学机制，为理解量子多体系统中低能构型涌现提供了新的物理视角，并为量子态工程中的低能子空间选择性制备开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Energy-ordered occupation is deeply embedded in quantum physics, from the Aufbau principle governing the filling of electronic states in atoms and molecules to the emergence of low-energy configurations in quantum many-body systems. However, the dynamical mechanism by which a generic quantum state develops such an energy hierarchy remains a fundamental question. Here we show that such an energy hierarchy can emerge dynamically from nonlinear quantum dissipation. Rather than being imposed as a principle or generated through coupling to a thermal reservoir, an Aufbau-like ordering of energy levels emerges intrinsically under quantum Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics from a generic initial mixed state. This convergence is a nontrivial consequence of Lyapunov monotonicity and instability of disordered population configurations. The resulting dynamics establish an intrinsic nonlinear mechanism for organizing density-matrix populations and provides a route toward selective preparation of low-energy subspaces. Numerical simulations confirm analytical predictions and illustrate convergence toward low-energy sectors.

</details>


### [111] [Bound-state-mediated remote charging of a quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26862)
*Jian-Jian Cheng,Hai-Bo Qiu,Lin Zhang,Ming-Liang Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维耦合腔阵列中利用原子-光子束缚态实现量子电池的远程充电。当量子比特的跃迁频率位于传播带外时，体系形成偶奇宇称束缚态，其能量劈裂驱动充电器到电池的相干能量转移，从而在抑制辐射泄漏的同时实现远程充电。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池的远程充电面临关键挑战：作为能量传输介质的腔模光子环境会引发辐射泄漏，导致激发能量耗散，阻碍高效的远程能量传输。如何抑制这种辐射损失并实现可靠的远程充电是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 构建一个由两个二能级系统（TLSs）组成的充电器-电池模型，分别局域耦合到一维耦合腔阵列的两个格点上。将TLS跃迁频率调节到传播带之外，使系统形成具有局域化光子成分的原子-光子束缚态。通过分析宇称分辨谱，研究偶奇宇称束缚态的能量劈裂及其对相干能量传输的驱动作用，并分析束缚态距带边的位置对交互距离、光子组分和光子损耗敏感性的影响。

Result: 带隙抑制了共振发射，局域束缚态可介导远程充电。充电时间由束缚态的能量劈裂决定，充电ergotropy与TLS布居在束缚态上的比例相关。靠近带边的束缚态能延长TLS的相互作用距离，但包含更大的光子组分，因此更易受到光子损失的影响。

Conclusion: 利用带隙外的原子-光子束缚态可以实现高效的量子电池远程充电机制，但需要在交互距离与光子损耗鲁棒性之间进行权衡——靠近带边的束缚态交互更远但更易损失。

Abstract: Remote charging of a quantum battery (QB) is hindered by radiative leakage of the excitation into the photonic environment that acts as a mediator for energy transfer. We consider a charger-battery model consisting of two two-level systems (TLSs) that are locally coupled to two sites of a one-dimensional coupled cavity array. When their transition frequency lies outside the propagation band, the system forms atom-photon bound states with localized photonic components, and the overlap of these components lifts the degeneracy of the even- and odd-parity bound states, yielding an energy splitting that drives coherent energy transfer from the charger to the QB. In this way, the band gap suppresses resonant emission and the localized bound states mediate remote charging. From the parity-resolved spectrum, we relate the charging time to the energy splitting and the charged ergotropy to the fraction of TLS population on the bound states. Bound states closer to the band edge extend the interaction range of the TLSs but contain a large photonic fraction and are consequently more susceptible to photon loss.

</details>


### [112] [Quantum Interconnects Part I: Strategic Quantum Network Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26886)
*Gustavo Castro do Amaral*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子网络的发展需要超越重复器、存储器和处理器等技术层面的框架，论证了当前量子网络缺乏效用驱动形成机制的原因不仅在于应用不成熟，更在于抽象不足——异构量子平台与功能紧密耦合，阻碍了技术无关效用函数的定义。为此提出一个包含物理平台、功能、服务、应用和用例的分层架构，并指出量子互连是解耦物理实现与网络功能的关键技术，从而为基于智能体的战略性网络形成奠定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典互联网在资源共享和通信需求的效用激励下演进，而量子网络目前缺乏能够产生类似激励的主导应用，导致网络形成主要由技术可行性、实验验证和预期未来价值驱动。作者认为这一问题的根源不仅是应用不成熟，更重要的是抽象层缺失，异构量子平台与功能紧耦合阻碍了效用函数的定义。

Method: 提出五层分层架构：物理平台（PP）、功能（F）、服务（S）、应用（A）和用例（UC），论证量子互连作为解耦物理实现与网络功能的使能技术，使得效用函数能够在功能层级定义，从而引入基于智能体的策略性网络形成机制。

Result: 论文论证了量子互连不仅是互操作性设备，更是战略性量子网络演化的基本使能器，通过分层抽象可以实现效用驱动的网络形成，并建立策略性网络形成的适用条件。

Conclusion: 量子网络的规模化演进需要从技术驱动转向效用驱动，而实现这一转变的关键在于通过量子互连实现物理平台与网络功能的解耦，配合分层架构定义技术无关的效用函数，从而使战略性网络形成成为可能。

Abstract: The realization of large-scale quantum networks requires more than advances in quantum repeaters, memories, and processors, it requires a framework explaining how heterogeneous quantum technologies evolve from isolated deployments into interconnected infrastructures. While the classical Internet evolved under strong utility incentives associated with resource sharing and communication demands, quantum networking currently lacks dominant applications capable of generating comparable incentives. As a consequence, contemporary quantum networks are largely formed through technology-driven decisions motivated by technical feasibility, experimental validation, and expected future value. This work argues that the absence of utility-driven network formation is not solely a consequence of immature applications, but also of insufficient abstraction. In particular, heterogeneous quantum platforms remain tightly coupled to the functionalities they provide, preventing the definition of technology-independent utility functions. A hierarchical architecture consisting of Physical Platforms (PP), Functionalities (F), Services (S), Applications (A), and Use-Cases (UC) is proposed, together with the argument that quantum interconnects constitute the enabling technology required to decouple physical implementations from network functionalities. Such decoupling permits the definition of utility functions at the functionality level and establishes the conditions under which strategic (agent-based) network formation becomes applicable. Quantum interconnects should therefore be viewed not only as interoperability devices, but also as fundamental enablers of strategic quantum network evolution.

</details>


### [113] [Correcting Connectivity in Arc-Based QUBO Models for Fixed-Fleet Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26894)
*Omer Gurevich,Maor Matityahu,Tal Mor,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了仅含度数（degree-only）的弧哈密顿量在固定车队、同质、无容量约束车辆路径问题（VRP）中的应用。由于局部惩罚仅定义环覆盖（cycle cover），基态可能包含与车场不连通的顾客环。作者构建了一个多项式规模的QUBO修复方案，采用带上限的单商品流（capped single-commodity flow），并证明了在显式惩罚假设下每个基态路由都是连通的且成本最优。编码规模、电路复杂度、经典基准审计和IQM Emerald量子硬件实验均被验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅度数弧哈密顿量是VRP的简洁QUBO表述，但其局部惩罚只能确保环覆盖，无法保证所有顾客环与车场连通。不连通的基态对应不可行的路由方案，这严重限制了该类哈密顿量的实际可用性。本文旨在通过构造多项式规模的QUBO修复方案，消除不连通基态，同时保持编码和电路实现的高效性。

Method: 1) 构造采用带上限单商品流的多项式规模QUBO修复方案，通过增加流约束确保所有顾客节点与车场相连；2) 在显式惩罚假设下证明每个基态路由连且成本最优；3) 分析两种编码方案：未约化编码使用|E|(1+⌈log2(N-K+1)⌉)个逻辑问题量子比特，可逆的实现采用compute–phase–uncompute方法，在O(N²logN+Nlog²N)逻辑门内评估流惩罚；4) 对比完整无环图上车场分隔的单序列位置编码与流模型的量子比特数和电路复杂度；5) 通过1,200个矩阵的经典基准和32,000次IQM Emerald量子任务进行验证。

Result: 未约化编码使用恰好|E|(1+⌈log2(N-K+1)⌉)个逻辑问题量子比特；可逆实现在完整图上使用O(logN)可重用工作空间和O(N²logN+Nlog²N)逻辑门。在完整无环图上，车场分隔单序列位置编码在流词长增长时使用更少问题量子比特和更少写入项；而流模型在常见可逆记账模型下取得更小的结构化逻辑门上界。量子硬件实验中，仅度数电路78.05%的p=1 shots产生无效的不连通基态，而14量子比特流增强电路无完全可行样本。

Conclusion: 带上限单商品流QUBO修复从理论上解决了仅度数弧哈密顿量的连通性问题，并且在经典审计中验证了公式化正确性。然而实际量子设备上的深度一（depth-one）termwise Ising电路实验显示，即便有流修复，也没有产生完全可行的样本，说明当前设备的编译和映射质量仍需改进；这些设备结果表征的是映射哈密顿量和编译过程，而非渐进路由解算的优势。

Abstract: We revisit a degree-only arc Hamiltonian for fixed-fleet, homogeneous, uncapacitated vehicle routing. Because its local penalties define only a cycle cover, ground states may contain customer cycles disconnected from the depot. We construct a polynomial-size quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) repair using capped single-commodity flow and prove that every ground-state routing is connected and cost-optimal under explicit penalty assumptions. For $N-1$ customers and $K$ nonempty routes, the unreduced encoding uses exactly $|E|(1+\lceil\log_2(N-K+1)\rceil)$ logical problem qubits. A reversible compute--phase--uncompute realization evaluates the flow penalties in $O(N^2\log N+N\log^2N)$ logical gates on a complete graph with $O(\log N)$ reusable workspace and no product register. On complete loopless graphs, a depot-delimited single-sequence position encoding uses fewer problem qubits and fewer written terms when the flow-word length grows. Conversely, the flow model achieves a smaller structured logical-gate upper bound under a common reversible accounting model. Exact audits of the Hamiltonian and circuit implementation, combined with a $1{,}200$-matrix classical benchmark, verify the formulation and quantify the connectivity gap. Finally, a 32,000-shot Amazon Braket task on IQM Emerald characterizes depth-one termwise Ising circuits on a diagnostic $N = 4,\, K = 1$ counterexample instance. In the degree-only circuit, $78.05\%$ of selected $p=1$ shots realize the invalid disconnected ground state; the reduced 14-qubit flow-augmented circuit yields no fully feasible sample. These device results characterize mapped Hamiltonians and compilation rather than an asymptotic routing solution advantage.

</details>


### [114] [Cavity-assisted homodyne detection with a single photodiode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26938)
*Jonas Junker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种腔辅助零差探测方案，利用阻抗匹配的行波腔，使量子场和本振光分别从不同端口进入，在GHz频段实现高效、低噪声的压缩态正交分量测量，同时抑制技术性本振边带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频压缩态对量子计量和信息处理至关重要，但GHz频段的正交分量测量一直具有挑战性。标准平衡零差探测（BHD）需要在两个光电探测通道中精确匹配复杂传递函数以抑制本振噪声，这在高频段越来越困难。而非对称单光电二极管零差探测虽避免了该匹配要求，但需要数百毫瓦的本振功率才能实现高信号效率和散粒噪声裕度，且无法抑制技术性本振边带。

Method: 提出腔辅助零差探测方案：量子场和本振光从阻抗匹配行波腔的分离端口进入。谐振本振载波透射到光电探测器，与非谐振的偏共振信号边带（由腔反射）拍频。同时，腔线宽之外的技术性本振边带被抑制。作者推导了包含腔内损耗的量子输入-输出关系和线性化光电流表达式。

Result: 推导结果表明，在中等入射本振功率下即可实现接近单位的信号传递效率。腔线宽和自由光谱范围决定了可用的探测带宽。

Conclusion: 腔辅助零差探测为高频（GHz级）量子正交测量提供了一种可行方案，兼顾了高信号效率、适中的本振功率需求以及技术本振边带抑制能力，为高频压缩态的应用提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: High-frequency squeezed states are important for quantum metrology and information processing, but quadrature measurements at gigahertz frequencies remain challenging. Balanced homodyne detection (BHD), the standard approach, requires closely matched complex transfer functions in both photodetection channels to suppress local-oscillator (LO) noise and maintain a well-defined readout quadrature. This matching becomes increasingly difficult at high bandwidths. Strongly asymmetric single-photodiode homodyne detection avoids this requirement, but achieving high signal efficiency and shot-noise clearance typically requires hundreds of milliwatts of LO power and does not suppress technical LO sidebands. I propose cavity-assisted homodyne detection, in which the quantum field and LO enter separate ports of an impedance-matched traveling-wave cavity. The resonant LO carrier is transmitted to the photodetector, where it beats with off-resonant signal sidebands reflected from the cavity. At the same time, technical LO sidebands outside the cavity linewidth are suppressed. I derive the quantum input-output relations and linearized photocurrent including intracavity loss, and show that near-unity signal-transfer efficiency can be achieved at moderate incident LO power. The cavity linewidth and free spectral range determine the usable detection band.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum-enhanced ghost imaging recognition via joint optimization of speckle patterns and quantum network parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26972)
*Yirui Mao,Xiangyu Ge,Yuhang Tu,Anqi Zhang,Le Wang,Shengmei Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于散斑图案与量子网络参数联合优化的鬼成像识别方法。利用经典卷积与散斑-物体点积运算的数学等价性，引入散斑一致性正则化实现光学编码与量子特征提取器的端到端联合优化，采用8量子位并行电路（块编码+星形纠缠结构）提取高阶特征。在1.5625%超低采样率下，MNIST和Fashion-MNIST数据集分别达到90.1%和81.7%的识别精度，较经典CNN提升2.6%，较传统混合量子机器学习模型最大提升14.2%，并在真实光学鬼成像系统上验证了84.8%的平均识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鬼成像在超低采样率下实现高保真识别仍具挑战；现有量子机器学习方法在识别精度和抗噪能力上表现不足。传统混合量子学习方法精度低、噪声鲁棒性差，需要一种能同时优化光学编码与量子特征提取的端到端方案。

Method: 提出散斑图案与量子网络参数联合优化框架：基于经典卷积与散斑-物体点积的数学等价性，引入散斑一致性正则化实现光学编码与量子特征提取器的端到端联合优化；设计并行的8量子位量子电路，采用块编码和星形纠缠结构从桶信号中提取更高阶特征。

Result: 在1.5625%超低采样率下，MNIST和Fashion-MNIST识别精度分别为90.1%和81.7%；较经典CNN提升2.6%，较传统混合QML模型最大提升14.2%；在真实光学鬼成像系统上平均识别精度达84.8%，并表现出对量子噪声的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 散斑图案与量子网络参数的联合优化为低采样鬼成像识别提供了可靠且实用的解决方案，验证了光学编码与量子特征提取协同设计的有效性。

Abstract: Ghost imaging enables nonlocal image reconstruction and exhibits strong robustness against interference, but achieving high-fidelity recognition at ultra-low sampling rates remains challenging. Quantum machine learning offers a novel approach for efficient feature extraction on noisy medium-scale quantum devices; however, existing methods generally suffer from low recognition accuracy and weak noise resistance. This paper proposes a ghost imaging recognition method based on the simultaneous optimization of speckle patterns and quantum network parameters. By leveraging the mathematical equivalence between classical convolution and speckle-object dot product operations in ghost imaging, a speckle consistency regularization mechanism is introduced to achieve end-to-end joint optimization of optical coding and quantum feature extractors. A parallel 8-qubit quantum circuit employing block coding and a star-shaped entanglement structure is designed to extract higher-order features from bucket signals. Simulation results on the MNIST and Fashion-MNIST datasets show that at an ultra-low sampling rate of 1.5625%, the proposed framework achieves recognition accuracies of 90.1% and 81.7%, respectively, representing a 2.6% improvement over classical convolutional neural networks and a maximum improvement of 14.2% over traditional hybrid quantum machine learning models. This method also exhibits strong robustness to quantum noise and has been validated on a real optical ghost imaging system, achieving an average recognition accuracy of 84.8%. These results confirm that the joint optimization of speckle patterns and quantum network parameters provides a reliable and practical solution for low-sampling ghost imaging recognition.

</details>


### [116] [Towards Reproducible Evaluation of Distributed Quantum Circuit Partitioning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27099)
*Javier Vela-Tambo,Davud Azizov,Tian Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出当前分布式量子计算（DQC）中电路划分启发式算法的评估仅关注总纠缠成本，忽略了网络约束带来的结构和时间开销。作者通过引入单机基准测试指标到分布式电路，并利用开源自动化评估流水线，系统评估了多种划分算法在不同量子网络拓扑上的表现，发现纠缠成本相近的划分算法可能在物理执行代价上差异巨大，强调全面的电路级指标对DQC编译器设计的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DQC研究中评估划分启发式算法主要集中于总纠缠成本，无法全面反映分布式网络约束带来的结构性和时间性开销，存在评估盲区。这种评估缺陷可能导致选择次优的划分策略，进而影响量子算法在分布式架构上的实际执行效率，因此需要更全面的电路级评估指标来指导DQC编译器的设计。

Method: 作者将成熟的单机基准测试指标应用于划分后的分布式电路，以量化网络约束对执行性能的影响。具体而言，构建了一个开源、自动化的评估流水线，系统性地在标准化工作负载和多种量子网络拓扑上评估各类划分算法，对比其物理执行代价。

Result: 经验结果显示，具有相似纠缠成本的划分算法可能引入截然不同的物理执行开销，包括电路深度严重增加和门密度显著降低等隐藏权衡。这表明单纯依赖纠缠成本不足以评估划分算法的优劣。

Conclusion: 全面的电路级指标对于引导未来DQC编译器的设计至关重要。分布式网络约束下的结构化开销（如电路深度和门密度）应被纳入划分算法的评估框架，以改进DQC的执行效率和编译优化。

Abstract: Distributed Quantum Computing (DQC) addresses the physical scaling limitations of monolithic quantum processors by networking modular Quantum Processing Units (QPUs). Efficient execution of quantum algorithms on DQC architectures requires compiling them across QPUs while minimizing inter-QPU communication bottlenecks, primarily through circuit partitioning. However, current evaluations of state-of-the-art partitioning heuristics focus primarily on the total entanglement cost of the partitions, failing to capture the broader structural and temporal overheads introduced by distributed network constraints. This paper addresses this evaluation gap by applying established monolithic benchmarking metrics to partitioned distributed circuits to quantify the performance impact of network constraints. Using an open-source, automated evaluation pipeline, we systematically assess diverse partitioning algorithms across standardized workloads and quantum network topologies. Our empirical results reveal that partitioning algorithms with comparable entanglement costs can still introduce drastically different physical execution penalties. By exposing these hidden trade-offs, such as severe increases in circuit depth and substantial reductions in gate density, this study demonstrates that comprehensive circuit-level metrics are essential for guiding the future design of DQC compilers.

</details>


### [117] [Kramers pseudospin and the quantum number proposed for the many-electron Dirac?Coulomb Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27112)
*Shadan Ghassemi Tabrizi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别出先前文献中提出的"单电子时间反演平方和"这一守恒量背后的算符本质——它实际上是附着于所选Kramers对的赝自旋的一个分量。通过角动量代数可以复现其整数本征值谱并得到本征矢的闭式解。然而，该算符与Dirac-Coulomb哈密顿量的对易关系并不成立，因此其作为量子数的合法性被否定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的文献[Phys. Rev. A 94, 052104 (2016)]提出单电子时间反演平方和是一个具有整数本征值谱的守恒量，并将其期望值用作Kramers污染的诊断工具。本文旨在揭示该构造背后的真正算符身份，并检验其作为守恒量子数的有效性。

Method: 作者识别出该算符为附着于所选Kramers对的一个赝自旋分量，利用角动量代数量子化规则复现了报告的本征值谱，并推导出本征矢量的闭式解析表达式。同时分析了标签k的物理含义——它是赝自旋投影到由基组固定轴上的量值的两倍，并考察了Kramers对重新定相时k的变化行为。

Result: 该算符的角动量代数确实能复现文献报告的本征值谱，但其与多电子Dirac-Coulomb哈密顿量的对易关系是不成立的。所提出的量子数-k²经不起推敲，因此不能作为合法的守恒量子数使用。

Conclusion: 该"守恒量"实际上只是一个赝自旋分量，其本征值谱的整数性源于角动量代数而非物理对称性。由于与哈密顿量不对易，它不能作为守恒量子数，相关诊断应用需要重新审视。

Abstract: The square of a sum of single-electron time reversals has been proposed as a conserved quantity with an integer eigenvalue spectrum for the many-electron Dirac-Coulomb Hamiltonian [Phys. Rev. A 94, 052104 (2016)], and its expectation value is in use as a diagnostic of Kramers contamination. This work identifies the operator behind the construction, a component of a pseudospin attached to the chosen Kramers pairs, whose angular-momentum algebra reproduces the reported spectrum and yields the eigenvectors in closed form. The label $k$ entering the proposed quantum number $-k^2$ is twice the magnitude of a pseudospin projection onto an axis fixed by the basis and changes when the Kramers pairs are rephased. The commutation with the Hamiltonian is unfounded, and the proposed quantum number does not stand.

</details>


### [118] [Optimal Gaussian networks for private distributed quantum sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27136)
*Hanbom Yoo,Byeongyun Yang,Hyunwoo Yoo,Seongjin Hong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在理论上刻画了满足完美本地隐私条件的高斯量子网络，证明双模压缩真空态是隐私保持高斯态的基本构建块，并解析推导了完美本地隐私下的最优灵敏度，实现了相对光子数和探测模式数的海森堡标度。数值实验验证了超越散粒噪声限的量子增强灵敏度，并证明该隐私条件在任意相位无关量子信道（包括光损耗）下均保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有分布式量子传感旨在估计授权的整体（集体）参数，同时防止对各个局域参数的独立估计。然而此前缺乏对满足这一完美本地隐私条件的高斯量子网络的系统性理论刻画，需要建立一般性框架来构建和优化隐私保持的连续变量量子传感网络。

Method: 利用高斯配对矩阵的图表示来分析高斯量子网络，解析刻画满足完美本地隐私的条件；在此基础上推导最优灵敏度的解析形式；通过局部零差测量和最大似然估计进行数值验证；分析相位无关量子信道下隐私条件的保持性。

Result: 揭示了双模压缩真空态是隐私保持高斯态的基本组成单元；解析推导出完美本地隐私下的最优灵敏度，并发现可实现同时针对光子数和传感模式数的海森堡标度；数值上验证了超越散粒噪声限的量子增强灵敏度；证明了本地隐私条件在任意相位无关量子信道（含光损耗）下依然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建和优化隐私保持的连续变量量子传感网络提供了一般的理论框架，从解析层面给出了完美本地隐私的完整刻画及其最优灵敏度的界限，为该领域后续研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Private distributed quantum sensing aims to estimate an authorized collective parameter while preventing independent estimation of individual local parameters. Here, we analytically characterize Gaussian quantum networks satisfying this perfect local privacy condition. Using a graph representation of the Gaussian pairing matrix, we show that two-mode squeezed vacuum states constitute the essential building blocks of privacy-preserving Gaussian states. We then analytically derive the optimal sensitivity under perfect local privacy and determine a Gaussian probe that achieves Heisenberg scaling with respect to both the photon number and the number of sensing modes. Using local homodyne measurements and maximum-likelihood estimation, we numerically verify quantum-enhanced sensitivity beyond the shot-noise limit while preserving perfect local privacy. We further show that the local-privacy condition is preserved under arbitrary phase-independent quantum channels, including optical loss. Our results provide a general framework for constructing and optimizing privacy-preserving continuous-variable quantum sensing networks.

</details>


### [119] [Dynamics and spectra of open quantum systems coupled to anharmonic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27163)
*Kian Damezin,Brendon W. Lovett,Erik M. Gauger,Adam Burgess*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了在开放量子系统中数值精确地建模非简谐环境的框架：通过匹配准则将非简谐环境映射为等效简谐环境，得到包含偏移裸谱和静态无序项的有效谱密度；同时针对少模阻尼非简谐环境（匹配准则失效情形）发展了一套研究框架，揭示了非简谐环境对系统动力学的独特影响，包括增强的非马尔可夫性、吸收谱中的新效应以及增强能量输运的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统开放量子系统研究通常将环境建模为简谐振子集合，但真实环境往往因非简谐性而表现出独特的非马尔可夫效应。目前缺乏数值精确地处理非简谐环境的理论框架，因此需要发展新方法来捕捉这类环境的真实物理效应。

Method: （1）提出匹配准则，将非简谐环境映射为等效简谐环境，从而获得有效的谱密度——由非等距能隙产生的偏移裸谱集合，以及一个来自浴耦合算符对角线非零方差的零频项（等效于静态无序）。（2）针对匹配准则因环境非高斯性而失效的情形（含少数阻尼非简谐模式的环境），另行发展了一套研究框架。

Result: 对于连续和离散阻尼非简谐环境，研究发现其独特影响包括：相比简谐近似具有增强的非马尔可夫性、吸收谱中出现新效应，以及非简谐环境可能增强能量输运。

Conclusion: 非简谐环境与简谐近似存在本质差异，必须超越简谐环境假设才能准确描述真实开放量子系统的动力学。本文提供的两种数值精确框架为研究此类系统铺平了道路，并揭示了非简谐性可作为调控系统动力学（如增强输运）的新自由度。

Abstract: In open quantum systems, the environment is typically modelled as a collection of harmonic oscillators, whereas realistic environments often exhibit unique non-Markovian effects due to the anharmonicity of the environment. Here we present a framework for modelling anharmonic environments in open quantum systems numerically exactly, in which, via a matching criterion, we map the anharmonic environment onto an effective harmonic one. This results in an effective spectral density that is a collection of shifted bare spectra arising from the non-equidistant energy gaps of the anharmonic environment, along with a unique zero-frequency term that effectively acts as static disorder on the system. This is due to the non-zero variance of the diagonal of the bath coupling operator. In tandem, we develop a framework for investigating the effects of environments comprising a few damped anharmonic modes where the matching criterion fails to work due to the non-Gaussianity of the environment. For both continuous and discrete damped anharmonic environments, this work investigates their unique influence on the system's dynamics, including enhanced non-Markovianity compared to a harmonic approximation, novel effects in the absorption spectrum, and the potential for anharmonic environments to enhance energy transport.

</details>


### [120] [Conditional contraction coefficients and their applications to quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27171)
*Christoph Hirche,Ian George,Theshani Nuradha,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了"条件收缩系数"这一新概念，通过减去参考系统中已有的可区分性，将收缩系数框架推广到包含任意量子参考系统的场景，并建立了一套统一的强数据处理不等式（SDPI）框架，涵盖迹距离、量子相对熵等度量，并应用于量子网络、量子存储和混合时间等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准收缩系数忽略辅助量子系统，而现有的完全收缩系数要求比较的状态具有相同的参考边缘态。这限制了收缩系数在量子网络等具有量子侧信息和分布式关联场景中的应用，需要一种更通用的框架来量化带量子参考系统的信息压缩。

Method: 本文引入条件收缩系数族，通过从总可区分度中减去参考系统已含有的可区分度来纳入任意量子参考系统。作者建立了包含SDPI常数、扩张系数和相对收缩系数的统一理论框架。对于迹距离，证明了优化可限制在正交输入态上；对于量子相对熵，证明了其条件收缩系数恰好等于条件互信息的收缩系数，将经典对应关系扩展到量子侧信息场景。还识别了发散度量所需的结构性质并讨论了一般化扩展。

Result: 建立了条件收缩系数的完整理论框架；证明了迹距离情形可简化为正交输入态优化；严格建立了量子相对熵收缩系数与条件互信息收缩系数之间的等式；推广了Polyanskiy-Wu关于互信息收缩的界限；为混合时间和量子存储的基本极限提供了新视角。

Conclusion: 该工作为分析量子网络设置中的信息压缩提供了统一框架，其中量子侧信息和分布式关联是信息处理任务的内在特征。这对于理解量子信道在分布式场景下的信息保持能力、量子存储的极限以及信息压缩的时间演化具有重要意义。

Abstract: Contraction coefficients quantify the loss of distinguishability induced by a channel and provide a strong form of the data-processing inequality. While standard contraction coefficients ignore auxiliary quantum systems, existing extensions based on complete contraction coefficients require the compared states to have identical reference marginals. In this work, we introduce conditional contraction coefficients, a novel family that incorporates arbitrary quantum reference systems by subtracting the distinguishability already present in the reference system. We develop a general framework for contraction coefficients with such quantum side information, including the corresponding strong-data-processing-inequality (SDPI) constants, expansion coefficients, and relative contraction coefficients. For the trace distance, we show that the optimization can be restricted to orthogonal input states. For the quantum relative entropy, we prove that its conditional contraction coefficient is exactly equal to the contraction coefficient of the conditional mutual information, extending the classical correspondence between relative-entropy contraction and mutual-information contraction to the setting with quantum side information. More generally, we identify structural properties of divergences required for these results and discuss extensions beyond the relative entropy. These results establish a unified framework for analyzing information contraction in quantum network settings, where quantum side information and distributed correlations are intrinsic features of the information-processing task. Applications include an extension of the Polyanskiy-Wu bounds on mutual information contraction, new perspectives on mixing times, and fundamental limits on quantum memories.

</details>


### [121] [Strong Converse Exponent of Quantum State Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27202)
*Mario Berta,Hao-Chung Cheng,Roberto Rubboli,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了量子态合并纠缠代价的强逆指数，证明其由优化后的α-ζ条件Rényi熵刻画（ζ=α/2∈[1/2,1]），与通常支配量子信息理论强逆指数的夹层条件Rényi熵形成对比。由此推导出净化距离下部分平滑条件最小熵的强逆指数，该指数由俱乐部夹层条件熵支配，而全局平滑则导致夹层表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态合并是量子信息论中的核心协议，理解其纠缠代价的强逆指数对于刻画协议在有限资源下的精确行为至关重要。此前的研究表明许多量子协议的强逆指数由夹层条件Rényi熵支配，但量子态合并是否遵循这一规律尚不清楚。

Method: 通过分析量子态合并纠缠代价的强逆极限，建立其与优化α-ζ条件Rényi熵（ζ=α/2）之间的联系；并进一步利用该结果推导部分平滑条件最小熵（在净化距离下）的强逆指数，揭示俱乐部夹层条件熵在其中所起的支配作用。

Result: 量子态合并的强逆指数由α-ζ条件Rényi熵取ζ=α/2时的优化形式刻画，而非通常的夹层条件Rényi熵。部分平滑（净化距离）条件下的条件最小熵强逆指数由俱乐部夹层条件熵支配，而全局平滑情形则归结为夹层表达式。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了量子态合并与大多数已知量子协议在强逆指数上的本质差异，确立了α-ζ条件Rényi熵在刻画强逆行为中的新角色，并为不同平滑方式下条件熵的强逆行为提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: We determine the strong converse exponent for the entanglement cost of quantum state merging, showing that it is characterized by the optimized $α$-$z$ conditional Rényi entropies with $z=α/2\in[1/2,1]$. This contrasts with the sandwiched conditional Rényi entropies that typically govern strong converse exponents in quantum information theory. As a consequence, we derive the strong converse exponent of the partially smoothed conditional min-entropy in purified distance. This exponent is governed by club-sandwiched conditional entropies, whereas global smoothing leads to a sandwiched expression.

</details>


### [122] [The Find Rows and Columns and Decode algorithm for quantum expander codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27211)
*Dimiter Ostrev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的量子扩展码解码算法，将经典的"查找擦除并解码"（Find Erasures and Decode）算法适配到量子扩展码上。该算法运行时间为线性，可并行化到对数深度，相比Small Set Flip和Small Set Find算法，无需考虑稳定子生成元的子集开销，需要的扩展性更低且能纠正更多错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子扩展码的解码是量子纠错的关键问题，现有的Small Set Flip和Small Set Find算法需要枚举稳定子生成元的子集，带来额外的计算开销，同时需要较强的扩展性条件，且纠错能力有限。因此需要一种更高效、更强大且更易满足条件的解码算法。

Method: 将经典纠错码中的Find Erasures and Decode（查找擦除并解码）算法进行适配和改进，应用于量子扩展码。新算法通过在量子设置下重新设计擦除查找与解码流程，避免了稳定子生成元子集的枚举开销，实现了线性时间复杂度，并利用并行化达到对数深度。

Result: 新算法在线性时间内运行且可并行化到对数深度；相比Small Set Flip和Small Set Find，它不需要考虑稳定子生成元的子集，所需的扩展条件更低（即更弱的扩展性要求即可保证正确解码），并且能够纠正更多类型的错误。

Conclusion: 该算法为量子扩展码提供了一种更高效、更强大的解码方案，降低了实现和解码的复杂性，在时间效率、扩展性要求和纠错能力三个方面都优于现有的Small Set Flip和Small Set Find方法。

Abstract: A new adaptation of the Find Erasures and Decode algorithm from classical to quantum expander codes is presented. It runs in linear time and is parallelizable to logarithmic depth. Compared to Small Set Flip and Small Set Find, the new algorithm avoids the overhead of considering the subsets of stabilizer generators, requires less expansion and corrects more errors.

</details>


### [123] [Tight Bounds for Purity and Product Testing from Partial Transposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27217)
*Oren Akresh,Jacob Beckey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在纯度和乘积测试两个任务中，正部分转置（PPT）松弛在渐近样本复杂度层面不会损失任何信息，且其下界可由非自适应单副本协议精确匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量多个拷贝的量子态能显著减少学习其性质所需的样本数，但相干测量在实验上难以实现。当前实验通常一次制备和测量一个副本，并可适应性调整后续测量。问题在于自适应性使得测量策略空间难以刻画，而PPT松弛提供了一种数学上易处理的更大类测量，但其代价是可能弱化下界。

Method: 本文考察PPT类的所有测量（包括直接测量的严格超集），证明在纯度和乘积测试这两个基本任务上，PPT松弛在渐近样本复杂度上不损失任何东西。证明仅需基本的对称子空间恒等式，提供了一种简洁路径来获得自适应单副本测量的严格下界。

Result: 在两个基本任务（纯度测试和乘积测试）中，针对完整PPT测量类的下界可由非自适应单副本协议精确匹配，即PPT松弛在渐近样本复杂度层面没有损失。

Conclusion: PPT松弛是一种强有力的工具，可用于推导自适应单副本测量的严格下界，且其简单性意味着该方法具有广泛的适用潜力。

Abstract: Coherent measurements across multiple copies of an unknown quantum state can substantially reduce the number of samples required to learn its properties, but remain experimentally challenging. Current experiments typically prepare and measure one copy at a time, potentially adapting later measurements to earlier outcomes. A central challenge is adaptivity, which makes the space of possible measurement strategies difficult to characterize. The positive-partial-transpose (PPT) relaxation bypasses this complexity by considering a larger, mathematically tractable class of measurements, at the risk of weakening the resulting bounds. Here we show, surprisingly, that the relaxation loses nothing at the level of asymptotic sample complexity for two fundamental tasks: purity testing and product testing. In both cases, lower bounds against the full class of PPT measurements are matched by nonadaptive single-copy protocols. Moreover, our proof requires only basic symmetric subspace identities, providing a simple route to sharp lower bounds for adaptive single-copy measurements.

</details>


### [124] [Efficient formation and identification of single emitters in 4H-SiC following maskless heavy ion implantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27281)
*S. Eserin,L. K. S. Zimmermann,E. Schneider,M. Ludlow,J. Heiler,O. G. Lloyd-Willard,K. Stockbridge,G. Aresta,L. Antwis,S. K. Clowes,M. Hofmann,S. Kucera,P. Berwian,F. Kaiser,B. N. Murdin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了在4H-SiC中通过无掩模重离子（铋和锡）低能注入形成孤立亮单发射体，退火后最多18%的注入位点拥有单发射体，并提出一种分级表征方案，通过强度阈值与光子关联测量快速筛选和识别发射体类型，无需耗时低温光谱，为室温量子技术提供高效路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳化硅（SiC）中的氧空位相关单光子发射体是前景广阔的量子技术平台，具有高光学亮度和强自旋读出对比度。然而，缺乏无掩模形成和快速识别这些发射体的方法，是阻碍其走向可扩展量子器件的一大障碍。

Method: 采用低能无掩模重离子（铋和锡）注入在4H-SiC中形成单发射体；退火温度在900-1000度C时获得最优产率（最高18%位点含单个发射体）；通过占位统计建模估算最大化单发射体产率的注入剂量；引入分级表征方案：先以简单强度阈值筛选单发射体候选，再通过光子关联测量确认，最后利用偏振、饱和计数率和磁共振频率的关联来判定发射体类型。

Result: 退火后可实现最高18%注入位点具有单发射体，900-1000度C是最优退火温度范围。分级表征方案证明可以不借助耗时低温光谱区分发射体类型，实现快速筛选与识别。

Conclusion: 无掩模重离子注入结合选择性筛选为室温量子技术（如量子传感）提供了一种高效形成和快速识别近表面单发射体的可行路线。

Abstract: Single photon emitters in silicon carbide (SiC) are a leading platform for scalable quantum technologies. Recent interest has focused on oxygen-vacancy-related emitters, which show exceptionally high optical brightness and strong spin readout contrast. One barrier to scalable quantum devices based on these emitters is the challenge of maskless formation and rapid identification. Here, we demonstrate the formation of isolated bright single emitters in 4H-SiC, using low-energy maskless implantation of heavy ions bismuth and tin. Following annealing, up to 18% of implanted sites host a single emitter, with optimal yields achieved at annealing temperatures of 900-1000 degrees C. Occupancy statistics are modelled to estimate the implantation dose that maximises single-emitter yield. We introduce a tiered characterisation scheme, where a simple intensity threshold isolates single-emitter candidates, confirmed through photon correlation measurements, after which correlations between polarisation, saturation count rate and magnetic resonance frequency assign emitter type. It is shown that time-consuming low-temperature spectroscopy is not necessary to distinguish emitter types. Together, maskless heavy-ion implantation and selective screening offer an efficient route to forming and rapidly identifying near-surface single emitters for room-temperature quantum technologies such as quantum sensing.

</details>


### [125] [Optimal cloning of mixed states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27298)
*Marco Fanizza,Dmitry Grinko,Thilo Scharnhorst,Jack Spilecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子态的近似克隆与近似转置问题，证明了对于秩为 r 的混合态，要实现高保真度的近似克隆或转置，所需拷贝数下界为 Ω(krd/ε)，与随机纯化信道给出的上界 O(krd/ε) 匹配，即 n = Θ(krd/ε) 是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Werner 的纯态克隆机已证明纯态场景下 n = Θ(kd/ε) 个拷贝足以克隆 k 个额外拷贝，而随机纯化信道给出了混合态克隆的上界 O(krd/ε)。一个自然的问题是：混合态场景下能否做得更好？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 作者通过研究投影子克隆（projector cloning）这一特殊情形来证明下界：输入态被承诺为 P/r 的形式，其中 P 是秩为 r 的正交投影子。通过分析该特殊情况，推导出混合态克隆所需拷贝数的下界 Ω(krd/ε)。同样的技术也被应用于近似转置问题。

Result: 证明了混合态近似克隆所需拷贝数的下界为 n = Ω(krd/ε)，与随机纯化信道达到的上界 O(krd/ε) 匹配，从而确立 n = Θ(krd/ε) 的最优复杂度。对于近似转置问题，同样证明了 n = Θ(krd/ε) 的拷贝数复杂度。

Conclusion: 混合态近似克隆和近似转置的最优样本复杂度均为 Θ(krd/ε)；随机纯化信道（结合 Werner 克隆机）在这两个任务上都是最优的，无法进一步改进。

Abstract: We consider the problem of approximate cloning of quantum states: given $n$ copies of an unknown state $ρ\in \mathbb{C}^{d \times d}$, prepare an $(n+k)$-copy state with high fidelity to $ρ^{\otimes (n+k)}$. Werner's pure state cloner is the optimal channel for the pure state case, and shows that $n = Θ(kd/\varepsilon)$ copies are necessary and sufficient to clone $k$ additional copies of an unknown pure state to fidelity $1-\varepsilon$. The random purification channel gives a straightforward extension of Werner's cloner to mixed state inputs: given $n$ copies of a mixed state, randomly purify your input, apply Werner's channel in the larger Hilbert space, and then trace out the auxiliary registers. This gives a mixed state cloner using $n = O(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies to clone rank-$r$ states. Can one do any better? We show that the answer is no: one must use $n = Ω(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies. We prove our lower bound by studying the special case of projector cloning, in which the input state $ρ$ is promised to be of the form $P/r$, where $P$ is a rank-$r$ orthogonal projector.
  As a further application of our techniques, we consider the closely related problem of approximate transposition of quantum states, where one seeks to convert $ρ^{\otimes n}$ to a $k$-copy state with high fidelity to $(ρ^T)^{\otimes k}$. Here, we again show $n = Θ(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies are necessary and sufficient for this task.

</details>


### [126] [Evaluating Quantum Kernel Methods for Track-Based Classification in High-Energy Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27333)
*Emmanuel Billias,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种大规模量子核分类的系统性设计，通过基于CLAS12漂移室特征的量子支持向量分类器（QSVC）对粒子径迹进行分类。采用六比特全纠缠ZZFeatureMap编码事件，在MPI多节点HPC上分布式构建核矩阵，实现了1.0×10^5训练样本和4.0×10^5测试样本的精确核矩阵构建，比先前高能物理量子核研究大一个数量级以上。在理想模拟下达到99.99%的最高召回率，但引入校准硬件噪声模型（FakeMumbaiV2）后AUC从0.9985降至0.9671，召回率仍保持99.51%。几何分析显示噪声使量子嵌入结构向最大混合态压缩但保持相对排序，证明了该工作流在HEP规模下的可扩展性和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高能物理（HEP）领域的量子核分类研究受到量子比特规模和核矩阵构建成本的限制，无法扩展到实际物理实验所需的大规模数据集。作者旨在克服这一瓶颈，设计一种可扩展的量子核分类工作流，在真实HEP数据规模下验证量子增强分类的潜力和硬件噪声的影响。

Method: 使用CLAS12漂移室数据，将每个事件经六比特全纠缠ZZFeatureMap编码为量子态，以态间保真度定义量子核并嵌入标准SVM框架。将态制备与核构建解耦，利用MPI多节点HPC分配分布式评估核矩阵。对比线性、多项式、RBF、sigmoid SVM核和极端随机树（ERT）基线，并在FakeMumbaiV2噪声模型下评估性能退化，同时进行量子嵌入的几何结构分析。

Result: 理想QSVC召回率达99.99%，为所有模型中最高；核矩阵构建规模达1.0×10^5训练/4.0×10^5测试事件，比先前HEP量子核研究大一个数量级以上。在FakeMumbaiV2噪声模型下（500训练/2,000测试事件），AUC从0.9985降至0.9671，峰值显著性提升从17.5降至约3.5，但召回率仍保持99.51%。几何分析显示噪声使嵌入结构向最大混合态压缩但保持相对排序。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在HEP相关规模下可扩展、可复现的量子核实验工作流，验证了量子核分类在信号保持方面的优势在硬件噪声下虽被削弱但未被消除。同时量化了现实硬件噪声对量子增强分类的实际代价，为后续量子机器学习在粒子物理中的应用提供了方法学基础。

Abstract: We present a systematic design for large-scale quantum kernel classification, demonstrated through a quantum support vector classifier (QSVC) for particle-track classification using centroid-based CLAS12 drift-chamber features. Each event is encoded into a six-qubit state via a fully entangled ZZFeatureMap, whose fidelities define a quantum kernel within a standard SVM framework. By decoupling state preparation from kernel construction and distributing evaluation across a multi-node MPI-based HPC allocation, the approach scales to 1.0x10^5 training and 4.0x10^5 test events with an exactly constructed kernel matrix, to our knowledge more than an order of magnitude larger than prior high-energy-physics quantum-kernel studies.
  Benchmarked against linear, polynomial, RBF, and sigmoid SVM kernels and extremely randomized trees (ERT), the ideal QSVC achieves the highest recall (99.99%) among all models. Under a calibrated hardware noise model (FakeMumbaiV2, 500 training / 2,000 test events), AUC falls from 0.9985 to 0.9671 and peak significance improvement falls from 17.5 to ~3.5, yet recall remains at 99.51% -- indicating this signal-retention advantage is attenuated but not eliminated by circuit-level decoherence.
  Geometric analysis of the quantum embedding shows near-orthogonal inter-class states with coherent intra-class neighborhoods under ideal simulation; under noise this structure compresses toward the maximally mixed state while preserving its relative ordering.
  These results demonstrate a scalable, reproducible workflow for quantum kernel experimentation at HEP-relevant scale, quantifying the practical cost of realistic hardware noise on quantum-enhanced classification.

</details>


### [127] [Dynamics-based nonclassicality witness under dissipative dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27337)
*Nguyen Vu Khoi Huynh,Nicky Nel Narido Labayna,Martine Schut,Valerio Scarani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Tsirelson提出的基于动力学的量子性认证测试从孤立谐振子扩展到与耗散耦合（热弛豫、纯退相和Caldeira-Leggett模型）的系统，利用Moyal-Wigner相空间形式证明耗散需要经典界限的耗散依赖移位，并给出了非经典性见证保持有效性的阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的Tsirelson动力学测试假设系统是孤立的，但在实际实验中这一假设无法满足，开放系统的耗散效应会破坏原始协议的适用性和有效性。

Method: 将Tsirelson的动力学测试扩展至耦合了热弛豫、纯退相和Caldeira-Leggett模型三种标准耗散模型的谐振子，利用Moyal-Wigner相空间量子力学形式体系进行分析。

Result: 证明了引入耗散后需要对经典界限进行与耗散相关的移位，并确定了非经典性见证保持有效性的耗散阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作为开放量子系统中基于动力学的量子性认证提供了理论框架，使得此类测试在实际实验条件下仍然适用。

Abstract: A dynamics-based test, originally proposed by Tsirelson for the harmonic oscillator, provides a method for certifying quantumness under the assumption of a known Hamiltonian. These tests, however, are typically proposed for isolated systems, an assumption that breaks down in experimental implementation. In this paper, we extend the protocol to the harmonic oscillator coupled to standard models of dissipation: thermal relaxation, pure dephasing, and the Caldeira--Leggett model. Using the Moyal-Wigner formalism of quantum mechanics in phase space, we show that the introduction of dissipation requires a dissipation-dependent shift of the classical bound, and provide the threshold under which the nonclassicality witness retains its validity.

</details>


### [128] [Randomness can be certified in energy-constrained semi-device-independent scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27357)
*Shashank Kumar Ranu,Lewis Wooltorton,Alastair A. Abbott,Omar Fawzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于能量约束的prepare-and-measure框架，通过将Navascués-Pironio-Acín（NPA）层级适应到允许设备间共享纠缠的能量约束场景，构造了猜测概率的半定规划松弛，证明确实在完全量子敌手存在下仍可进行认证随机数生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能量约束SDI框架的安全性分析假设制备与测量设备间最多共享经典关联，但近期研究表明预先分发纠缠的敌手可发动更强攻击，将可提取随机性降至此前认证的速率之下，因此核心开放问题是：在该场景下随机性是否仍能被认证？

Method: 将Navascués-Pironio-Acín（NPA）层级方法适配到能量约束设置中，构造猜测概率的半定规划（SDP）松弛。该方法不限制制备与测量设备间共享量子态的维度，仅依赖能量约束这一假设。

Result: 所构造的半定规划松弛为可提取随机性提供了经过认证的下界，并在一定能量值范围内证明这些下界严格为正，从而率先生成可在可提取随机性认证的理论可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作回答了开放问题：即使在完全量子的敌手（设备间共享任意维度的纠缠态）存在下，基于能量约束的SDI框架仍能实现认证随机数生成，具有正的安全余量。

Abstract: The prepare-and-measure framework based on energy constraints offers a practical middle ground between fully device-dependent and device-independent quantum cryptography. The only assumption on an otherwise uncharacterized prepare-and-measure device is that the energy of the prepared states is bounded. Existing security analyses of this framework assume that the preparation and measurement devices share at most classical correlations, and under this assumption certified lower bounds on the extractable randomness have been established. Recent work has shown that an adversary who pre-distributes entanglement between the devices can mount attacks that are strictly more powerful than those available when the devices share only classical correlations, reducing the extractable randomness below the previously certified rates. This leaves open the fundamental question of whether randomness can be certified at all in this scenario.
  We address this open question by constructing semidefinite programming relaxations of the guessing probability by adapting the Navascués-Pironio-Acín hierarchy to the energy-constrained setting where shared entanglement between the devices is permitted. Our relaxations yield certified lower bounds on the extractable randomness without enforcing any restrictions on the dimensions of the quantum state shared between the preparation and measurement devices. We show that these certified lower bounds are strictly positive for a range of energy values, thereby answering the open question affirmatively: certified randomness generation is theoretically possible in the energy-constrained SDI framework even in the presence of a fully quantum adversary.

</details>


### [129] [Factorized Boolean representations for efficient quantum synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27430)
*Mehul Shah,Robert Fiszer,Marek Perkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现最小化的布尔表达式仍保留代数结构优化无法触及的结构（项之间的包含与互补极性关系），提取这些结构虽然增加操作数，却能生成执行成本更低的量子电路；关键指标不是电路操作数而是最宽操作的控制数（超线性成本），共享因子提取可将少量宽操作转为大量窄操作并减少量子比特数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设最小化布尔表达式后映射到电路是最优的，但这一假设忽略了最小化形式中仍保留的代数结构（包含关系和互补极性关系）。量子电路执行成本的关键并非操作数，而是最宽操作的控制数，这是一个超线性成本。因此需要探索最小化之外的结构化表示能否进一步降低实际执行成本。

Method: 作者提出一种表示级变换方法：从最小化的布尔表达式中提取项之间因包含关系和互补极性关系而形成代数结构，通过共享因子提取将少量宽操作（高控制数）转换为大量窄操作（低控制数），从而降低超线性成本。该方法在表示层面保证两种成本度量（控制数和量子比特数）均不增加；随后翻译为可执行电路时需解除辅助线（uncompute），但仍保留优势。

Result: 在量子搜索和因式分解算法的基准与预言机测试中，表示级变换从不增加任一成本度量。翻译为电路后部分优势被归还（因辅助线需反计算），但分解后的电路仍能让领先的电路级优化器达到比未经辅助时更低的最终计数，且速度更快。

Conclusion: 计算的表示本身是一种资源，可以在编译前进行优化，这既不同于逻辑最小化，也不同于电路级优化。最小化形式并非最佳映射起点，提取代数结构可以获得执行成本更低的电路。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms promise advantages beyond classical reach, but running them on error-corrected hardware requires translating Boolean specifications into reversible circuits, and the resources that translation demands determine what is executable. Established methods minimize a Boolean expression and map it to a circuit, assuming the minimized form is best. Here we show that minimized expressions retain algebraic structure minimization cannot reach, arising from containment and complementary-polarity relationships among their terms, and that extracting it yields circuits cheaper to execute despite having more operations. The decisive quantity is not a circuit's operation count but the control count of its widest operation, a superlinear cost; extracting shared factors trades a few wide operations for many narrow ones and reduces qubit count. Across benchmarks and oracles from quantum search and factoring algorithms, at the representation level the transformation never increases either cost measure, a guarantee from its construction. Translation to an executable circuit returns part of that advantage, since auxiliary lines must be uncomputed, yet the factorized circuit still left a leading circuit-level optimizer reaching lower final counts, and faster, than unaided. The representation of a computation is therefore itself a resource, optimizable before compilation and distinct from both logic minimization and circuit-level optimization.

</details>
