### [130] [Large Deviations for the Bose--Einstein Condensate of the Ideal Gas in the Canonical Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26378)
*Andreas Deuchert,Xuanyu Li*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究正则系综中由一般单粒子哈密顿量描述的无相互作用玻色气体中玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体粒子数的大偏差估计，发现左右尾部概率以不同指数速率衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚中粒子数涨落的大偏差行为，补充已有关于粒子数分布平均和方差的结果，揭示左右尾部非对称的指数衰减规律。

Method: 针对正则系综下一般单粒子哈密顿量的非相互作用玻色气体，采用大偏差理论的方法，分别分析凝聚体粒子数左尾和右尾的概率衰减速率。

Result: 证明了当粒子数趋于无穷时，凝聚体粒子数分布的左尾和右尾概率以不同的指数速率衰减，表明大偏差行为具有方向不对称性。

Conclusion: 在一般性单粒子哈密顿量框架下，玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚粒子数的大偏差呈现非对称指数衰减，为深入理解凝聚体涨落统计特性提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: We consider a non-interacting Bose gas governed by a general one-particle Hamiltonian in the canonical ensemble. Our main results are large-deviation estimates for the number of particles in the Bose--Einstein condensate. We show that the left- and right-tail probabilities decay at different exponential rates as the number of particles tends to infinity.

</details>


### [131] [A closure criterion for electromagnetic curves on the sphere in a uniform ambient field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26412)
*C S López-Monsalvo*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究均匀背景磁场中单位球面上带电测试粒子的运动，利用Noether定理的推广将问题化为求积，推导出旋转数为第三类完全椭圆积分，并基于此建立了闭合轨迹的判定准则及其与无量纲比值（半回旋频率/速度）的关系，还计算了Mane严格临界值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球面上带电粒子在均匀磁场中的运动，不仅是一个经典的力学问题，还因其与接触几何、Reeb轨道等现代数学理论的深刻联系而具有重要价值。作者希望通过精确数学分析来刻画轨迹闭合条件，并揭示其与无量纲参数的依赖关系。

Method: 利用扩展的Noether定理（适用于含磁力系统）将球面上带电粒子的运动约化为求积分问题；将轨迹在一个纬度振荡中获得的方位角（旋转数）表示为Legendre形式的第三类完全椭圆积分；通过对旋转数关于半回旋频率求导来符号化地分析其单调性；利用万尼尔流（exact magnetic flow）的概念计算Mane严格临界值，并在临界值以上将闭合轨迹与闭合Reeb轨道建立联系。

Result: 轨迹闭合当且仅当旋转数是整圈的合理数倍，该准则在每个第一积分水平集上成立。旋转数仅依赖于一个无量纲比值（半回旋频率与速度之比），闭合轨迹由此固定该比值。作者给出了携带给定有理缠绕数的比值数量，证明了极点在单一水平集上可达（此时运动退化为单摆），并计算了系统的Mane严格临界值，在临界值以上的闭合轨迹是显式接触形式的闭合Reeb轨道。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了球面均匀磁场中带电粒子运动的完整几何与动力学刻画：闭合性判据被归结为旋转数（椭圆积分）的有理性，系统参数被约化为单一无量纲比值，且揭示了闭合轨迹与接触几何中Reeb轨道之间的紧密联系。

Abstract: We consider the motion of a charged test particle confined to the round unit sphere in a uniform ambient magnetic field. We use an extension of Noether's theorem for systems with magnetic forces to reduce the problem to a quadrature. The rotation number of a trajectory, the azimuth it gains over one oscillation in latitude, is a complete elliptic integral of the third kind in Legendre form. A trajectory closes if and only if that advance is a rational multiple of a full turn. The criterion holds on every level set of the two first integrals. Then, we differentiate the rotation number with respect to the half-cyclotron frequency and sign that derivative on each of the two branches into which a level set divides. The rotation number sees the charge, the mass, the field and the speed through a single dimensionless ratio, that of the half-cyclotron frequency to the speed. A closed trajectory therefore fixes a value of that ratio. We say how many values carry a given rational winding. The poles are attainable on a single level set, where the motion reduces to a pendulum. The field has no flux through the sphere, so it is globally exact. We compute the Mañé strict critical value of the resulting exact magnetic flow. Above that value the closed trajectories are closed Reeb orbits of an explicit contact form.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum cellular automata and invertible phases of matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26456)
*Corey Jones,Nikita Sopenko,Ryan Thorngren*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究定义在一致局部有限度量空间上的（费米子和玻色子）可逆准局域代数，证明了其Brauer等价类群与可逆态相群、量子蜂窝自动机的稳定等价类群同构。进一步利用对称幺半范畴的K-理论提出Kitaev猜测的Ω-谱定义，并通过c=1/2手征Majorana费米子网和(E8)_1共形网构造了非平凡的可逆准局域代数，从而在Z²上给出非平凡可逆态和量子蜂窝自动机的显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆相（invertible phases）的严格数学定义是凝聚态物理和拓扑相分类中的核心问题。Kitaev猜测存在一个Ω-谱对应可逆相的完整分类，但此前缺乏严格的数学框架。本文将可逆准局域代数、可逆态和量子蜂窝自动机三者统一起来，为可逆相的拓扑分类提供严格的算子代数基础。

Method: 采用算子代数方法，引入无限维局域von Neumann代数的可逆准局域代数的Brauer等价类群；利用对称幺半范畴中可逆准局域代数与有界扩张同构的K-理论来构造Ω-谱。通过c=1/2手征Majorana费米子网和(E8)_1共形网具体构造非平凡Brauer类，并利用有理对角共形场论时间切片网的可逆性证明任何全纯共形网离散化的可逆性。

Result: 建立了Brauer等价类群、可逆态相群和量子蜂窝自动机稳定等价类群三者之间的同构关系；构造了Kitaev所猜测的Ω-谱；证明了c=1/2手征Majorana费米子网与(E8)_1共形网给出非平凡的Brauer类，进而在Z²上得到非平凡可逆态和量子蜂窝自动机的显式例；证明了有理对角共形场论的时间切片网存在晶格自由度，全纯共形网的离散化可逆。

Conclusion: 该工作为可逆拓扑相分类建立了严格的算子代数框架，统一了可逆准局域代数、可逆态和量子蜂窝自动机三个视角，并为Kitaev的Ω-谱猜想提供了具体定义。通过共形场论构造明确展示了非平凡的二维可逆相例子，推进了可逆相的数学分类理论。

Abstract: We introduce and study (fermionic and bosonic) invertible quasi-local algebras over uniformly locally finite metric spaces $X$ with infinite-dimensional local von Neumann algebras. We show that the group of Brauer equivalence classes of such algebras is isomorphic to both the group of phases of invertible states and the group of stable equivalence classes of quantum cellular automata over $X\times \mathbb{Z}$. Using K-theory of the symmetric monoidal category of invertible quasi-local algebras and bounded spread isomorphisms, we propose a definition of an $Ω$-spectrum of invertible phases as conjectured by Kitaev. We then show that the $c=\frac{1}{2}$ chiral Majorana fermion net and the $(E_{8})_{1}$ conformal net provide Brauer non-trivial invertible quasi-local algebras, thus providing explicit constructions of non-trivial invertible states and quantum cellular automata on $\mathbb{Z}^{2}$. In addition, we show that the time-slice nets of rational diagonal conformal field theories admit lattice degrees of freedom, which implies the discretization of any holomorphic conformal net is invertible.

</details>


### [133] [Dynamical classical-field limit of Bosonic Gibbs states: Renormalized Hartree NLS correlations in 2D and 3D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26477)
*Phan Thành Nam,Rongchan Zhu,Xiangchan Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从玻色多体吉布斯动力学推导了环面T^d (d=2,3)上重整化Hartree NLS方程的时间依赖关联函数。与一维问题不同，高维情形下缩放量子粒子数不是一致有界的，经典场需要Wick重整化。证明结合了加权Hilbert空间中量子生成元的收敛性以及以Gibbs测度为主导的正解的Liouville方程的唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将玻色多体量子系统的吉布斯动力学与经典极限（Hartree NLS方程）联系起来是数学物理的核心问题。此前仅有1D结果（Fröhlich, Knowles, Schlein, Sohinger），高维情形因粒子数非一致有界及Wick重整化的需要而更具挑战性，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 通过两个关键步骤的组合：(1) 在加权Hilbert空间中证明量子生成元（many-body Schrödinger generator）向经典Liouville生成元收敛；(2) 证明以Gibbs测度为主导的正Liouville方程解的适当唯一性，从而确保极限的有效性。

Result: 证明了在d=2,3维环面上，玻色多体吉布斯动力学在合理时间尺度下收敛到重整化Hartree NLS方程的关联函数，克服了高维中Wick重整化与粒子数非一致有界带来的技术困难。

Conclusion: 该结果将1D理论推广到更高维度，确立了高维玻色多体系统吉布斯动力学的经典Hartree极限，为理解高维量子场论中的经典极限提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: In this paper we derive the time-dependent correlation functions of the renormalized Hartree NLS equation on the torus $\mathbb T^d$, $d=2,3$, from the corresponding bosonic many-body Gibbs dynamics. In contrast with the 1D problem studied earlier by Fröhlich, Knowles, Schlein and Sohinger, in higher dimensions the scaled quantum particle number is not uniformly bounded and the relevant classical fields require Wick renormalization. Our proof combines convergence of the quantum generators in weighted Hilbert spaces with uniqueness for positive solutions of the limiting Liouville equation that are dominated by the Gibbs measure.

</details>


### [134] [The Sharp Spectral Transition for Almost Mathieu Operators via Alternating Resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26590)
*Jiawei He,Xueyin Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意给定的频率共振指数和相位共振指数，总是存在一个频率和相位精确实现这些值，使得Almost Mathieu算子在ln|λ|>max{β(α),δ(α,θ)}条件下表现出Anderson局域化，解决了一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文献MR4686650中提出的猜想，该猜想涉及Almost Mathieu算子的Anderson局域化条件与频率共振指数β(α)和相位共振指数δ(α,θ)之间的关系。

Method: 通过构造性地证明对任意给定的频率共振指数β和相位共振指数δ，总能找到精确实现这两个数值的频率α和相位θ，使得Almost Mathieu算子在该参数下的Anderson局域化成立。

Result: 证明了当ln|λ|>max{β(α),δ(α,θ)}时，存在频率和相位精确实现给定共振指数，且Almost Mathieu算子在该条件下表现出Anderson局域化。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了文献MR4686650中的猜想，完善了Almost Mathieu算子Anderson局域化条件与共振指数之间关系的理论。

Abstract: We prove that for any given frequency resonance exponent and phase resonance exponent, there exist a frequency and a phase exactly realizing these values such that the Almost Mathieu operator exhibits Anderson localization for $\ln|λ|>\max\{β(α),δ(α,θ)\}$. This resolves the conjecture in \cite{MR4686650}.

</details>


### [135] [Coupled stochastic variational principles for multiscale surface gravity waves -- Part I: theoretical framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27074)
*Etienne Mémin,Arnaud Debussche*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个用于表面重力波的随机变分框架，从Luke变分原理出发，将速度势分解为确定性大尺度成分和正则化随机噪声项，通过逐路径变分原理得到随机拉普拉斯、伯努利和运动学边界条件，并通过期望变分原理闭合系统，保留Hamiltonian结构，为海洋波随机约化模型和动力学理论提供严格基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋表面重力波中未被解析的小尺度过程对可预测的大尺度波浪运动有重要影响，但传统的确定性变分框架（如Luke变分原理和Zakharov-Craig-Sulem公式）无法自然纳入随机性和不确定性的描述。因此需要开发一个统一的随机变分框架，为海洋波的随机约化建模提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 本文采用双层次变分方法：(1) 将Luke变分原理中的速度势分解为确定性大尺度分量和正则化随机噪声项，通过逐路径（path-wise）变分原理推导出随机形式的Laplace方程、Bernoulli方程和运动学边界条件；(2) 通过第二个期望意义下的变分原理闭合系统，得到噪声相关函数的演化方程。随后利用WKB近似分析显式解，并证明噪声相关函数满足带射线追踪动力学的Hamilton-Jacobi方程。

Result: 所得到的耦合系统保持了Zakharov-Craig-Sulem公式的Hamiltonian结构；通过WKB分析表明噪声相关函数满足Hamilton-Jacobi方程并具有射线追踪动力学特性，为后续的动力学理论（kinetic theory）和海洋波随机约化模型提供了严格的理论基础。

Conclusion: 该随机变分框架为表面重力波的不确定性量化提供了严格的数学基础，既保持了物理系统的Hamiltonian结构，又通过噪声相关函数的演化方程实现了系统的闭合，为减少海洋波模型及动能理论的后续发展奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a comprehensive stochastic variational framework for surface gravity waves. Starting from Luke's variational principle for irrotational, incompressible free-surface flow, we introduce a decomposition of the velocity potential into a large-scale deterministic component and a regularized stochastic noise term representing unresolved scales. A path-wise variational principle yields the stochastic counterparts of the Laplace, Bernoulli, and kinematic boundary conditions. To close the system, a second variational principle in expectation is formulated, providing evolution equations for the noise correlation functions. The resulting coupled system preserves the Hamiltonian structure of the Zakharov--Craig--Sulem formulation. We further analyze explicit solutions via WKB approximation and show that the noise correlation functions satisfy a Hamilton-Jacobi equation with ray-tracing dynamics. This framework provides a rigorous foundation for reduced stochastic models of ocean waves and for the kinetic theory developed in the companion paper.

</details>


### [136] [Quasi-polynomiality and $N$-point functions of single connected leaky completed Hurwitz numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27109)
*Chongyu Wang,Chenglang Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了单连通k-渗漏(r+1)-完备Hurwitz数的结构，证明在提取显式的Pochhammer型因子乘积后，固定亏格下稳定的连通渗漏Hurwitz数是关于[μ_i]模k+r的商的多项式，并给出了单连通渗漏Hurwitz数生成函数的闭式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究渗漏Hurwitz数的结构性质，特别是其关于分区μ的代数结构（多项式性）以及亏格依赖性，为理解和计算这类组合不变量提供理论基础。

Method: 首先通过提取显式的Pochhammer型因子，证明固定亏格下稳定连通渗漏Hurwitz数关于[μ_i]模k+r的商具有多项式结构；随后推导单连通渗漏Hurwitz数生成函数的闭式公式，利用该公式分析亏格依赖性。

Result: 证明了固定亏格下，提取Pochhammer型因子后，稳定连通渗漏Hurwitz数是关于[μ_i]模k+r的分式的多项式；并获得了生成函数的闭式公式，可用于研究亏格依赖关系。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了渗漏Hurwitz数的代数结构，建立了多项式性与闭式生成函数两大核心结果，为后续研究其亏格行为及进一步应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structures of single connected $k$-leaky $(r+1)$-completed Hurwitz numbers. We first prove that, for fixed genus, after extracting an explicit product of Pochhammer type factors, the stable connected leaky Hurwitz numbers for partition $μ$ are polynomials in the quotients $[μ_i]$ modulo $k+r$. We then give a closed formula for the generating function of single connected leaky Hurwitz numbers, which can be used to study the genus dependence of leaky Hurwitz numbers.

</details>


### [137] [Multiscale Loop Vertex Expansion for Cumulants, the $φ^4_2$ Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27188)
*Vincent Rivasseau*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了φ⁴₂模型（二维φ⁴量子场论模型），使用多尺度圈顶点展开方法（改进的构造性场论），证明了累积量在有限阶内的解析性和Borel可和性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: φ⁴₂模型被称为最简单的非平凡超可重整化模型，是构造性量子场论中重要的测试平台。作者希望改进已有的构造性场论工具，以获得该模型更严格的数学结果。

Method: 采用多尺度圈顶点展开（multiscale loop vertex expansion），这是对构造性场论方法的改进，通过圈顶点展开与多尺度分析相结合的技术来处理模型。

Result: 证明了该模型累积量在有限阶内的解析性，并进一步证明了其Borel可和性。

Conclusion: 多尺度圈顶点展开方法成功应用于φ⁴₂模型，在有限阶范围内得到了累积量的解析性和Borel可和性这一强数学性质，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: We consider the $φ^4_2$ model. It has been called the simplest non- trivial super-renormalisable model. The method we use is the multiscale loop vertex expansion, an improvement of constructive field theory. We prove analyticity and Borel summability of the cumulants up to a finite order.

</details>


### [138] [2-Morita Theory of $E_2$-Algebras and Module Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27228)
*Rongge Xu,Holiverse Yang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于先前E2-代数的2-Morita等价工作，建立了不同维数拓扑序的Morita等价的系统框架，通过n-Morita范畴$\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})$统一了各种n-Morita等价概念，并对n=1,2证明了范畴等价关系，引入了bi-bimodule概念统一描述局部模和受限模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑序在不同维度中的Morita等价缺乏统一的系统框架，已有的各种n-Morita等价概念分散且不统一，需要建立系统的数学框架来理解和刻画不同维数拓扑序之间的等价关系及其缺陷结构。

Method: 作者构建了n-Morita范畴$\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})$作为统一框架，基于E_n-代数的2-Morita等价的先验工作，比较了Haugseng与Gwilliam-Scheimbauer两种高维Morita范畴构造方式的不同；对n=1,2证明了$\mathrm{Mod}_n:\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})\to \mathrm{Mrt}_{E_{n-1}}(\mathrm{LMod}^{\mathrm{rep}}(\mathcal{C}))$是范畴等价；在该框架中自然引出bi-bimodule概念并给出其配向数据和融合规则。

Result: 建立了统一拓扑序Morita等价的n-Morita范畴框架，证明了低维情形下模块范畴实现与代数描述之间的等价性，并通过bi-bimodule及其融合规则厘清了凝聚现象产生的缺陷之间的关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解不同维度拓扑序的Morita等价提供了统一的范畴论语言，bi-bimodule的统一描述揭示了局域模与受限模的深层联系，为拓扑序缺陷结构的系统研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Building on our previous work on 2-Morita equivalence for $E_2$-algebras using topological pictures, we develop a systematic framework for Morita equivalence of topological orders in different dimensions in terms of $n$-Morita categories $\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})$. In this framework, various notions of $n$-Morita equivalence are unified as equivalences of objects in $\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})$. We also compare the constructions of higher Morita categories due to Haugseng and to Gwilliam--Scheimbauer.
  For $n=1,2$, we prove that the functor $\mathrm{Mod}_n:\mathrm{Mrt}_{E_n}(\mathcal{C})\to \mathrm{Mrt}_{E_{n-1}}(\mathrm{LMod}^{\mathrm{rep}}(\mathcal{C}))$ is an equivalence, relating the algebraic description of higher Morita theory to its realization in terms of module categories. In this formulation, the notion of a bi-bimodule arises naturally and provides a common framework for local and confined modules. Its explicit orientation data, together with the corresponding fusion rules, clarifies the relations among defects arising from condensation in topological orders.

</details>

### [139] [Crudely Estimated Maximum Mass for a Cosmological Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26203)
*Don N. Page*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文基于N体模拟，研究了超大质量黑洞双星在引力波辐射主导之前的旋入速率，发现轨道相对速度平方$(v/c)^2$随时间近似线性增加，速率约为210 Gyr的倒数（约为宇宙年龄的15倍），且与双星质量几乎无关。这本身不足以使双星在现今发生并合，但与引力辐射共同作用后，可使质量高达约$1.4\times10^{14} M_\odot$的等质量黑洞双星发生并合，这可被视为当前宇宙中黑洞质量的粗略上限。同时暗示并合产生的引力波应变在波周期超过约10万年后应减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超大质量黑洞双星在引力波辐射主导之前的动力学演化（即轨道旋入速率），并在此基础上推断黑洞并合的可能性上限以及引力波信号的频谱特征。

Method: 采用M. Milosavljevic和D. Merritt的N体数值模拟方法，分析近圆轨道超大质量黑洞双星的旋入率，并结合引力辐射理论计算并合条件。

Result: 发现$(v/c)^2$以约210 Gyr倒数的速率线性增长且与双星质量无关；与引力辐射结合后可支持最高约$1.4\times10^{14} M_\odot$的等质量双星并合；引力波应变在周期大于约10万年的波段应呈下降趋势。

Conclusion: 引力辐射是促成超大质量黑洞双星最终并合的关键因素，约$1.4\times10^{14} M_\odot$可作为当前宇宙黑洞质量的上限估计，并合引力波信号的应变在长周期波段应减小。

Abstract: $N$-body simulations by M.~Milosavljevic and D.~Merritt \cite{Milosavljevic:2001vi} suggest that for supermassive black hole binaries in their expected nearly circular orbits, before gravitational wave emission dominates, the inspiral rate causes the dimensionless orbital relative velocity squared, $(v/c)^2$, to increase nearly linearly with time at a rate that is approximately the inverse of 210 Gyr (about 15 times the present age of the universe), nearly independently of the mass of the binary. This by itself would not lead to coalescence by the present time, but when it is augmented by gravitational radiation, it can lead to coalescence for equal-mass black hole binaries up to about $1.4\times 10^{14} M_\odot$, which might then be conjectured to be a very crude upper limit to the mass of black holes in our universe at the present time. This also suggests that the gravitational wave strain from coalescing supermassive black hole binaries should decrease for wave periods greater than about 100\,000 years.

</details>


### [140] [Validating Timing-Model Accuracy for Continuous Gravitational Waves: A Comparison of LALSuite and PINT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26271)
*Kartikey Sharma,Reinhard Prix,Maria Alessandra Papa,Curt Cutler*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对LALSuite连续引力波计时模型与高精度脉冲星计时软件PINT进行了系统性验证。推导了计时误差与信号功率损失的定量关系μ≈(2πf)²σ₍τ₎²，并发现旧版爱因斯坦延迟实现导致约2.3微秒的计时偏差，而新版实现可将总偏差降至31纳秒以下，主要受限于LALSuite使用近似地球自转模型造成的Rømer延迟误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的计时模型是连续引力波探测和表征的关键，因为信号相位追踪依赖于精确计时。LALSuite作为广泛使用的连续引力波搜索工具，其计时模型需要与高精度参考软件（如PINT）进行系统性比对验证，以量化计时不精确性对探测灵敏度的实际影响。

Method: 本文首先推导了计时误差方差与信号功率分数损失之间的理论关系式μ≈(2πf)²σ₍τ₎²，然后分别比较LALSuite与PINT在太阳系组件（包括爱因斯坦延迟、Rømer延迟等）和双星组件上的计时模型差异。双星延迟测试使用474个编目双星脉冲星的轨道参数，同时验证了LALSuite源时导数的自洽性，并讨论了仅对引力波相关的太阳内部Shapiro延迟。

Result: 使用原始爱因斯坦延迟实现时，总不一致性约为σ₍τ₎≈2.3微秒（在1000Hz处对应μ≈0.02%）；使用新版爱因斯坦延迟实现后，一年内的总偏差降至σ₍τ₎≤31纳秒（在1000Hz处μ≤4×10⁻⁸），主导误差来源变为LALSuite近似的非均匀地球自转模型导致的观测站Rømer延迟误差。

Conclusion: LALSuite新版爱因斯坦延迟实现大幅提升了计时精度，其残余计时误差对连续引力波探测的影响已可忽略（μ≤4×10⁻⁸），但观测站Rømer延迟的地球自转近似成为新的主要误差来源。整体而言，LALSuite计时模型与PINT在所需精度上高度一致，足以支持连续引力波搜索。

Abstract: We present results of a systematic validation of the \lalsuite{} timing model for continuous gravitational waves against \pint{}, a modern high-accuracy pulsar-timing package. An accurate timing model is essential for tracking the signal phase, and hence for detecting and accurately characterizing continuous gravitational waves. In order to quantify the impact of timing inaccuracies, we derive and validate the leading-order relation $μ\approx (2πf)^2\stddtau^2$, where $μ$ is the fractional loss of signal power, $f$ is the signal frequency, and $\stddtau^2$ is the variance of the timing errors. We then compare the solar-system and binary components of the \lalsuite{} timing model against the corresponding models in \pint{}. With the original \lalsuite{} Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement is dominated by that component and has $\stddtau\simeq\SI{2.3}{\micro\second}$ (corresponding to $μ\simeq\SI{0.02}{\percent}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$). With the newer Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement (over one year) drops to $\stddtau\lesssim\SI{31}{\nano\second}$ (or $μ\lesssim\num{4e-8}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$) and is dominated by the observatory contribution to the \Romer{} delay, owing to the approximate Earth-rotation model used by \lalsuite{}. We additionally test binary delays using orbital parameters from \num{474} catalogued binary pulsars and verify the self-consistency of the \lalsuite{} source-time derivatives. Finally, we derive and discuss the \lalsuite{} Shapiro delay for signals passing through the solar interior, a case only relevant to gravitational waves.

</details>


### [141] [Effects of the interaction of dark matter and neutron-star matter on extreme and intermediate mass-ratio inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26274)
*Benjamin A. Wade,Julian Heeck,David A. Nichols*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在致密暗物质分布中的极端及中等质量比旋进系统中，暗物质与自身或普通物质的相互作用对引力波信号的影响。通过考虑不同的暗物质模型（尖峰或湮灭平台分布）、中子星次级的吸积以及质量变化，发现这些相互作用会产生独特的引力波特征，其中一些场景可以被天基引力波探测器区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究仅假设暗物质具有纯引力相互作用，而忽略了暗物质自湮灭或与重子/轻子物质的相互作用。本文旨在探索引入这些非引力相互作用后，是否会产生可被天基探测器观测到的独特引力波特征，从而更全面地揭示暗物质的本质。

Method: 研究考虑了宽泛的暗物质模型类别，包括改变暗物质分布（尖峰或湮灭平台）的模型，以及允许次级天体（中子星）在旋进过程中吸积暗物质并导致质量增加或保持静态的模型，同时计入动力摩擦的纯引力效应，分析这些因素对轨道动力学和引力波发射的影响。

Result: 研究发现暗物质与自身或普通物质的相互作用以及自湮灭会产生独特的引力波特征；在部分场景下，这些特征对于天基引力波探测器而言足够显著，可将其与纯引力情形区分开来。

Conclusion: 暗物质的非引力相互作用（自湮灭、与普通物质相互作用、吸积导致的质量变化）会在极端及中等质量比旋进系统的引力波信号中留下可观测的印记，这为利用天基引力波探测器探测和鉴别暗物质模型提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Extreme and intermediate mass-ratio inspirals in a dense dark-matter distribution have the effects of the dark matter imprinted on the orbital dynamics of and the emitted gravitational waves from these systems. Prior work has shown that space-based gravitational-wave detectors can measure the dark-matter-induced effects on the gravitational waves, which would give evidence for the presence of dark matter around the massive black hole. In this earlier work, the dark matter has been assumed to have only gravitational interactions (namely, no dark-matter self-annihilation or interactions between dark matter and ordinary baryonic or leptonic matter). In this article, we investigate the gravitational-wave effects of introducing such interactions of dark matter with itself or with ordinary matter in binaries with a neutron-star secondary. We consider broad classes of dark-matter models that change the distribution of dark matter (spikes or annihilation plateaus) and which permit accretion onto the secondary, as well as an increasing or static mass of the neutron star during the inspiral (in addition to the purely gravitational effect of dynamical friction). We find distinctive gravitational-wave signatures of these interactions and of self-annihilation, which in some of the scenarios could be sufficiently large for space-based detectors to distinguish them using gravitational-wave observations of these systems.

</details>


### [142] [Born Geometry, Metaparticles, and an Effective Geometric Realization of the Modular Black-Hole Remnant: A Phase-Space Resolution of the Schwarzschild Singularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26413)
*Paul-Robert Chouha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Born几何、模空间与metaparticle动力学框架下，构建了修正的Schwarzschild时空几何，将经典黑洞奇点替换为有限大小的模核心，从而实现了metaparticle黑洞热力学所预言的模残骸（modular remnant）的几何对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于metaparticle黑洞热力学的工作预言了蒸发终点存在稳定的冷模残骸，具有最小地平面积和最大Hawking温度，但对应的时空几何未知。本文旨在通过Born几何框架构建该模残骸的有效几何实现。

Method: 从坐标与对偶坐标构成的双重相空间出发，推导出Born不变径向距离所支配的Born-双重Schwarzschild几何；利用模不确定关系阻止在相空间原点的任意局域化，用有限模核心替代经典奇点；引入metaparticle对偶约束，诱导Born几何背景的共形变形并产生有效应力-能量张量，在核心附近导致径向与强能量条件的局域破坏。

Result: 获得一个有限各向异性源的内部区域，具有张力主导的径向分量。在模核心附近自然产生径向NEC和SEC的局域破坏，从而规避了Hawking-Penrose奇点定理的聚焦假设。该几何与metaparticle热力学预言的模残骸有效对应，替代了经典Schwarzschild奇点。

Conclusion: Schwarzschild奇点被一个有限的Born几何相空间结构所取代，其紫外行为由对偶的非几何自由度所支配，为模残骸提供了有效的几何实现。

Abstract: Recent work on metaparticle black-hole thermodynamics suggested the existence of a finite evaporation endpoint characterized by a minimal horizon area, a maximal Hawking temperature, and a stable cold modular remnant. However, the corresponding spacetime geometry remained unknown. In this work we construct an effective geometric realization of the modular remnant within the framework of Born geometry, modular spacetime, and metaparticle dynamics. Starting from a doubled phase-space description in which spacetime coordinates are supplemented by dual coordinates, we derive a Born-doubled Schwarzschild geometry governed by a Born-invariant radial distance. The associated modular uncertainty relation prevents arbitrary localization at the phase-space origin, rendering the classical Schwarzschild singularity physically inaccessible and replacing it with a finite modular core. We then incorporate the metaparticle duality constraint, which induces an effective conformal deformation of the Born-geometric background and generates a nontrivial effective stress-energy tensor. The resulting interior region develops a finite anisotropic source characterized by a tension-dominated radial sector. Localized violations of the radial Null Energy Condition and the Strong Energy Condition arise naturally near the modular core, providing a mechanism through which the focusing assumptions underlying the Hawking--Penrose singularity theorems are evaded. These results establish an effective geometric counterpart of the modular remnant inferred from metaparticle black-hole thermodynamics and suggest that the Schwarzschild singularity is replaced by a finite Born-geometric phase-space structure whose ultraviolet behavior is governed by dual, non-geometric degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [143] [Gödel-type universes in Lorentz-violating Kalb-Ramond gravity with Ricci- and Riemann-tensor nonminimal couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26472)
*Fernando M. Belchior*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在自发破缺洛伦兹对称性的Kalb-Ramond双形式引力模型中的齐次Gödel型几何。通过引入Ricci张量和Riemann张量两种非最小曲率耦合项，推导出了简化场方程，并重点分析了保持旋转平面对称性的赝磁场背景B12=b。结果表明Riemann耦合可以移动时序边界和闭类时曲线的临界半径，甚至允许因果临界值m²=4ω²，但普通正能量物质严重限制了完全因果解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索洛伦兹对称性自发破缺（通过Kalb-Ramond双形式的真空期望值实现）对Gödel型时空几何和因果性结构的影响，特别是研究非最小曲率耦合中Riemann张量耦合如何改变通常bumblebee模型中的结果，以及是否可能恢复因果性。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量基础上补充两项非最小曲率耦合：Ricci张量耦合B_a^c B_bc R^ab和Riemann张量耦合B^ab B^cd R_abcd。在齐次真空扇形中，令Kalb-Ramond场强和对称性破缺势的一阶导数消失，将场方程简化为Gödel型度规标架中的封闭代数系统，并推导不同取向下的曲率张量、约化度规方程和自洽方程。

Result: 对于赝磁场背景B12=b，Kalb-Ramond方程给出m²/ω²=2(ξ1+3ξ2)/(ξ1+2ξ2)。仅含Ricci耦合时恢复非因果的Gödel值m²=2ω²；加入Riemann耦合后，当ξ2=-ξ1时可实现因果临界值m²=4ω²，同时Riemann耦合可移动闭类时曲线的临界半径。此外讨论了完美流体、标量场和电磁场源。

Conclusion: Riemann张量非最小耦合为Gödel型时空的因果性修复提供了新的机制，理论上可以实现完全因果的解，但正能量条件会严重限制这类解的存在性。该工作为洛伦兹破缺引力理论中的时序结构研究提供了系统性分析框架。

Abstract: This paper studies homogeneous \Godel-type geometries in a gravitational model where local Lorentz symmetry is spontaneously broken by the vacuum expectation value of an antisymmetric Kalb-Ramond two-form. We consider the Einstein-Hilbert action supplemented by two independent nonminimal curvature couplings: a Ricci-tensor coupling of the form $B_{a}{}^{c}B_{bc}R^{ab}$ and a Riemann-tensor coupling of the form $B^{ab}B^{cd}R_{abcd}$. In the homogeneous vacuum sector, where the Kalb-Ramond field strength and the first derivatives of the symmetry-breaking potential vanish, the field equations reduce to a closed algebraic system in the tetrad frame of the \Godel-type metric. We derive the curvature tensors, the reduced metric equations, and the Kalb-Ramond consistency equations for several inequivalent orientations of the vacuum two-form. Particular attention is given to the pseudo-magnetic background $B_{12}=b$, because it preserves the symmetry of the rotation plane and yields a transparent deformation of the usual bumblebee result. For this sector the Kalb-Ramond equation fixes $\frac{m^2}{ω^2}=\frac{2(ξ_1+3ξ_2)}{ξ_1+2ξ_2}$, where $ξ_1$ and $ξ_2$ are the Ricci and Riemann couplings. The Ricci coupling alone reproduces the noncausal \Godel value $m^2=2ω^2$, while the Riemann coupling shifts the chronology bound and allows the critical causal value $m^2=4ω^2$ for $ξ_2=-ξ_1$. We also show that the Riemann coupling can move the critical radius for closed timelike curves, but ordinary positive-energy matter severely restricts completely causal solutions. Finally, we discuss perfect fluid, scalar field, and electromagnetic sources.

</details>


### [144] [Comparison of Two-Atom Cross Spectra in de Sitter Spacetime, Uniformly Accelerated Minkowski Vacuum, and a Thermal Bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26554)
*Zhiming Huang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个相同的二能级原子与四维无质量共形耦合标量场的弱耦合问题，比较了三种具有相同局部温度但物理来源不同的设置：de Sitter时空Bunch-Davies真空中共动原子、Minkowski真空中横向分离的匀加速原子、以及Minkowski热浴中的静态原子。当a=H且β=2π/H时，三种情况的局部Wightman核和局部光谱完全一致；但在有限分离下，真实热浴产生sinc(ΩL)空间因子，而de Sitter和横向加速真空产生双曲几何因子；长距离极限下，前两者交叉关联按L^{-2}衰减，后者仅按L^{-1}衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同物理机制（引力效应de Sitter时空、加速度效应Unruh效应、热浴环境）在相同局部温度下是否会产生相同的两原子量子关联模式，从而区分这些看似等效的量子场论真空/热态配置。

Method: 推导三种设置下两原子的交叉功率谱（cross spectra），并通过零分离极限统一获得单原子局部光谱；分别计算有限分离和长距离极限下的交叉关联行为，对比其空间衰减特性。

Result: 在a=H、β=2π/H条件下，三种配置的局部Wightman核和局部光谱完全一致；但有限分离时热浴产生sinc(ΩL)空间因子，而de Sitter和横向加速真空产生双曲几何因子；长距离时前者衰减为L^{-2}，热浴仅为L^{-1}；de Sitter与横向加速真空的交叉谱在瞬时匹配条件下完全重合。

Conclusion: 尽管三种配置具有相同的局部温度和局部光谱，它们在两原子空间关联上存在本质区别，可通过有限分离的交叉关联实验加以区分；de Sitter时空与横向加速真空在匹配条件下表现出相同的几何关联结构，而真实热浴的空间关联形态截然不同。

Abstract: We study the weak coupling of two identical two-level atoms to a four-dimensional massless conformally coupled scalar field and compare three settings with the same local temperature but different physical origins: comoving atoms in the Bunch--Davies vacuum of de Sitter spacetime, transversely separated uniformly accelerated atoms in the Minkowski vacuum, and static atoms in a Minkowski thermal bath. We first derive the two-atom cross spectra in the three settings and then obtain the single-atom local spectra uniformly from the zero-separation limit. When $a=H$ and $β=2π/H$, the complete local Wightman kernels and local spectra are identical in all three cases. At finite separation, a genuine thermal bath produces a $\sinc(ΩL)$ spatial factor, whereas de Sitter spacetime and the transversely accelerated vacuum produce a hyperbolic geometric factor. In the long-distance limit, the cross correlations in de Sitter spacetime and the transversely accelerated vacuum decay as $L^{-2}$, whereas those in the Minkowski thermal bath decay only as $L^{-1}$. The de Sitter and transversely accelerated vacuum cross spectra coincide when $a=H$ and the separations are instantaneously matched. For a fixed experimental setup, the physical separation between the two comoving atoms in de Sitter space evolves with cosmic expansion, whereas the pulled-back cross correlations in the uniformly accelerated and thermal Minkowski configurations are stationary.

</details>


### [145] [Relativistic Modeling for Solid Earth Tide Estimation via Space-to-Ground Clock Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26672)
*Qin Li,Wei-Hang Sun,Yu-Jie Tan,Cheng-Gang Qin,Jun Ke,Xiang-Pei Liu,Han-Ning Dai,Tong Liu,Cheng-Gang Shao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究利用空间-地面高精度时钟比对链路探测固体地球潮汐参数的可行性，通过三链路多普勒消除配置隔离目标信号，发现高轨道高度导致Love数间存在严重共线性，但h₂和k₂的有效组合参数可在30天观测窗口内稳定收敛，且当前提取精度主要受限于时钟稳定性而非径向精密定轨误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代原子钟技术的高精度使其从计时工具演变为引力势传感器，催生了相对论大地测量学这一交叉领域。鉴于高精度时钟网络具备探测周期性引力变化的潜力，有必要评估其在复杂观测噪声存在下、通过空间-地面链路反演固体地球潮汐参数的能力。

Method: 将地球引力势、日月直接潮汐势和固体地球潮汐效应纳入空间-地面时钟比较的高精度相对论框架；采用三链路多普勒消除配置隔离目标信号；对倾斜地球同步轨道卫星进行数值模拟，分析时钟不稳定性和有色精密定轨误差对参数提取的影响。

Result: 高轨道高度导致单个Love数(h₂和k₂)之间存在严重共线性，但二者组合的有效参数可在30天连续观测窗口内稳定收敛。敏感性分析表明，提取精度当前主要受限于时钟稳定性，而非径向精密定轨误差。

Conclusion: 尽管高轨道下单个Love数的反演存在共线性问题，但通过参数组合策略可以实现在30天内对有效潮汐参数的稳定估计，且当前精度瓶颈在于时钟稳定性，为未来高精度时钟网络用于相对论大地测量提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: With the rapid development of modern atomic clock technology, their unprecedented precision elevates them from timekeeping tools to gravitational potential sensors, thereby fostering the highly interdisciplinary field of Relativistic Geodesy. Given the potential for high-precision clock networks to detect periodic gravitational variations, it is imperative to assess their capability to invert solid Earth tide parameters via space-to-ground links in the presence of complex observational noise. To this end, we incorporate Earth's gravitational potential, direct lunisolar tidal potentials, and solid Earth tide effects into a high-precision relativistic framework for space-to-ground clock comparisons. By employing a three-link Doppler cancellation configuration to isolate the target signal, we perform numerical simulations for an inclined geosynchronous orbit satellite to analyze the effects of clock instability and colored precise orbit determination errors on parameter extraction. Our findings reveal that while high orbital altitudes cause severe collinearity between individual Love numbers, an effective parameter combining the $h_2$ and $k_2$ Love numbers successfully converges to a stable estimate within a 30-day continuous observation window. Furthermore, sensitivity analysis demonstrates that extraction accuracy is currently limited by clock stability rather than radial precise orbit determination errors.

</details>


### [146] [Near-Horizon BMS Symmetry and Implications on Black Hole Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26725)
*Nihar Ranjan Ghosh,Malay K. Nandy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态Schwarzschild黑洞近视界BMS类超平移对称性在热力学描述中的作用，构建了保持视界结构的微分同胚，将超平移参数视为Goldstone模式，并通过Einstein-Hilbert作用量的展开获取了有效作用量和守恒的视界荷。结果表明Bekenstein-Hawking面积律在领头阶恢复，次领头修正依赖于超平移扇区和黑洞动力学演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞物理中，近视界对称性的热力学意义仍是一个重要的开放问题，特别是在黑洞蒸发和信息恢复的背景下。该工作旨在揭示视界适应BMS类超平移在动态Schwarzschild黑洞热力学描述中的角色，探索超出静态极限的修正效应。

Method: 在近视界Rindler坐标系中构造保持视界结构的微分同胚类，将超平移参数提升为视界对称性破缺产生的Goldstone模式；围绕背景几何展开Einstein-Hilbert作用量获取Goldstone模式的有效作用量；使用Kodama矢量构造计算动态时空的表面引力，视界定义为未来外俘获视界；通过Noether荷推导熵。

Result: 视界超平移模式对视界表面引力有非平凡贡献，修改了超出静态极限的黑洞热力学描述；由Noether荷推导的熵在领头阶恢复Bekenstein-Hawking面积律，而次领头修正显式依赖于超平移扇区和黑洞动力学演化。

Conclusion: 近视界BMS类超平移模式动态地参与黑洞热力学描述，不仅改变表面引力，还引入超越面积律的次领头修正，这为理解黑洞蒸发和信息恢复中对称性的热力学角色提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Thermodynamic significance of near-horizon symmetries remains an important open question in black hole physics, particularly in the context of black hole evaporation and information recovery. In this work, we investigate the role of horizon-adapted Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs (BMS)-like supertranslations in the thermodynamic description of a dynamical Schwarzschild black hole. Working in a near-horizon Rindler coordinate system, we construct a class of diffeomorphisms that preserve the horizon structure and promote the associated supertranslation parameter to a Goldstone-like mode arising from the breaking of horizon symmetry. By expanding the Einstein-Hilbert action around the background geometry, we obtain the effective action for the Goldstone mode and identify the corresponding conserved horizon charge from the surface contribution of the action. The relevant horizon is defined at the future outer trapping horizon, while the surface gravity is computed using the Kodama-vector construction appropriate for dynamical spacetimes. We show that the horizon supertranslation mode contributes non-trivially to the surface gravity and modifies the thermodynamic description of the black hole beyond the stationary limit. Using the associated Noether charge, we derive the entropy of the horizon-BMS transformed geometry and find that the Bekenstein-Hawking area law is recovered at leading order, while subleading corrections depend explicitly on the supertranslation sector and the dynamical evolution of the black hole.

</details>


### [147] [Gravitational Lensing of Hayward Black Holes with EFT-Corrected Photon Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26785)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有效场论（EFT）修正光子传播条件下，Hayward正则黑洞能否通过强引力透镜效应在观测上与Schwarzschild黑洞区分开来。研究表明，尽管EFT修正参数化很小，但在临界传播区域附近其效应可被增强，强透镜可观测量能留下特征印记，且当Hayward参数接近临界值时修正更加显著，暗示强引力透镜可能成为区分两类黑洞的手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hayward正则黑洞作为Schwarzschild黑洞的一种替代模型，其观测特征是否与后者可区分是一个重要问题。常规透镜分析可能不足以区分两者，因此需要考虑EFT修正（电磁场与时空曲率的非最小耦合）这一额外自由度，以寻找可观测的判别信号。

Method: 作者首先推导了由电磁场与时空曲率非最小耦合导致的修正光子传播定律，然后在Hayward时空中分析修正后的光子轨迹。利用强偏折极限方法，计算了光子球及偏折角对数发散部分的修正，并针对代表性的Hayward参数值评估了强偏折可观测量（如爱因斯坦环半径、偏折角等），与Schwarzschild情形进行了系统对比。

Result: EFT修正对光子传播的效应可以在强透镜观测量上留下特征性印记。随着Hayward参数g³接近临界值，EFT修正的贡献变得更加显著，说明曲率依赖的光子传播修正近临界区域尤为相关。

Conclusion: 强引力透镜观测有望作为区分Hayward正则黑洞与Schwarzschild黑洞的潜在手段，特别是在近临界参数区间，EFT修正效应会放大，提高了可观测区分的可能性。

Abstract: We investigate whether a Hayward regular black hole can be observationally distinguished from a Schwarzschild black hole through strong gravitational lensing when effective field theory (EFT) corrections to photon propagation are taken into account. We derive the modified photon propagation law induced by non-minimal couplings between the electromagnetic field and spacetime curvature, and analyze the resulting photon trajectories in the Hayward spacetime. Using the strong deflection limit, we derive the corrections to the photon sphere and the logarithmically divergent part of the deflection angle. Although the EFT corrections are parametrically small, their effects can be enhanced near the critical propagation region, where the deflection angle exhibits a logarithmic divergence. We evaluate the strong-deflection observables for representative values of the Hayward parameter and compare them with the Schwarzschild case. We find that EFT corrections to photon propagation can leave characteristic imprints on strong-lensing observables. Furthermore, the contribution of the EFT corrections becomes more pronounced as the Hayward parameter $g^3$ approaches its critical value, indicating that curvature-dependent corrections to photon propagation can become particularly relevant in the near-critical regime. These results suggest that strong gravitational lensing may provide a means of distinguishing Hayward regular black holes from Schwarzschild black holes.

</details>


### [148] [Perturbations of black holes with primary hair: time evolutions, quasinormal modes and greybody factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26984)
*Georgios Antoniou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有平移对称性的超越Horndeski引力中带主标量毛的黑洞的引力扰动，重点关注轴向扇区，在物理度规和有效度规两种描述下进行时域演化，并在频域中探索准正规模（QNM）谱，计算灰体因子和吸收截面，最后通过有效势的局域形变来诊断QNM频率和灰体因子可观测量的相对稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越Horndeski引力中的标量毛黑洞提供了超越广义相对论的黑洞解，但其引力扰动性质（特别是准正规模谱和辐射特性）尚未被充分探索。理解这些黑洞的响应特性对于区分不同毛解、验证现有近似计算以及评估可观测量的稳健性具有重要意义。

Method: 聚焦轴向扇区的引力扰动，研究扰动方程的几何结构；在物理度规和有效度规两种描述下进行时域演化以量化不同毛解的响应差异；在频域中基于物理度规公式化探索QNM谱；计算灰体因子和吸收截面；利用有效势的局域形变作为诊断手段评估QNM频率和灰体因子可观测量的相对稳定性。

Result: 验证并扩展了已有基于有效度规方法得到的近似计算结果；量化了不同标量毛解之间的响应特征差异；给出了对应的灰体因子和吸收截面；通过有效势局域形变诊断了QNM频率和灰体因子可观测量对势形状的相对敏感性。

Conclusion: 该研究系统地建立了超越Horndeski引力中标量毛黑洞的轴向引力扰动分析框架，弥合了物理度规与有效度规描述之间的差距，并为区分不同毛解提供了时域和频域的可观测特征诊断工具。

Abstract: In this work we study gravitational perturbations of black holes carrying primary scalar hair in shift-symmetric beyond-Horndeski gravity. By focusing on the axial sector, we study the geometric structure of the perturbation equations and perform time-domain evolutions both in the physical and in the effective metric descriptions, in order to quantify differences in the response characteristics of solutions with different hair. In the frequency domain, we then explore the QNM spectrum in the physical-metric formulation, thus verifying and extending existing approximate calculations derived in the effective-metric approach. We also compute the corresponding greybody factors and absorption cross-sections. Finally, we use localized deformations of the effective potential as a diagnostic of the relative stability of quasinormal frequencies and greybody-factor observables.

</details>


### [149] [Conformal Cyclic Cosmology from Varying Fundamental Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27014)
*Konrad Marosek,Adam Balcerzak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究一种双度量宇宙学模型，其中引力与物质扇区由不同的度量描述，通过时间依赖函数α(t)相关联，导致引力常数G(t)∝α⁻³和引力传播速度c_grav(t)∝α⁻¹随时间演化。对于α在有限宇宙时间内趋于无穷的解，系统最终进入强奇点（G→0，c_grav→0），该奇点具有与循环宇宙学兼容的共形结构，并提出弱化引力可能导致引力束缚结构解体和黑洞视界收缩，最终过渡到辐射主导的终态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力参数随时间变化的宇宙学模型，特别是引力相互作用减弱（G→0、c_grav→0）的最终奇点结构，为康福循环宇宙学（CCC）提供可能的微观机制，并激励将分析推广到更一般的标量-张量引力理论。

Method: 采用双度量（bimetric）框架，设定引力度规与物质度规通过时间依赖函数α(t)关联；利用解析可解的特定解分析有限共形时间处的最终奇点行为，并运用Tipler和Królak判据评估奇点的强度。

Result: 证明该模型中最终奇点发生在有限共形时间处，其共形结构允许循环宇宙学之间的可能过渡；Tipler和Królak判据表明该奇点是强奇点。弱化引力会导致引力束缚结构解体、黑洞视界收缩，最终可能趋于辐射主导的终态。

Conclusion: 该双度量模型及其最终奇点的性质为康福循环宇宙学提供了理论支持，并为进一步推广到更一般的标量-张量引力理论提供了动机。

Abstract: We investigate a bimetric cosmological model in which the gravitational and matter sectors are described by distinct metrics related through a time-dependent function $α(t)$. This relation leads to dynamical gravitational parameters, with $G(t)\proptoα^{-3}$ and $c_{\rm grav}(t)\proptoα^{-1}$, where $c_{\rm grav}$ denotes the propagation speed of gravitational interactions. We consider a class of solutions for which $α\rightarrow\infty$ at a finite cosmic time, resulting in $G\rightarrow0$ and $c_{\rm grav}\rightarrow0$. For an analytically tractable solution, we show that the final singularity occurs at finite conformal time and possesses a conformal structure compatible with a possible transition between successive cosmological cycles. The Tipler and Królak criteria indicate that the singularity is strong. We further discuss the physical consequences of the weakening gravitational interaction, including the dissolution of gravitationally bound structures and the shrinking of black-hole horizons. These effects suggest a possible mechanism leading toward an effectively radiation-dominated final state, which may be relevant for Conformal Cyclic Cosmology. The results provide a motivation for extending the analysis to more general scalar--tensor theories of gravity.

</details>


### [150] [Electric and magnetic Penrose processes, charged-particle collisions and superradiance around a Lorentz-violating dyonic black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27293)
*Fernando M. Belchior,Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类Lorentz破缺的dyonic Kalb-Ramond黑洞中的电磁能量提取、带电粒子碰撞、带电场超辐射以及视界稳定性问题。该几何与dyonic Reissner-Nordström解不同，具有修正的渐近归一化和结合电、磁扇区的有效电荷。研究表明电/磁Penrose过程可通过负能量碎片坠入视界、另一碎片携带增强能量逃逸来实现，负能量态来源于电磁正则能量而非几何能层；非极端同向碰撞能量有限，而迎头或近临界构型可产生大质心能量，极端极限下满足径向可达条件时可能出现Bañados-Silk-West发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Lorentz对称性破缺背景下黑洞的电磁能量提取机制及其与标准Reissner-Nordström解的差异，探索电扇区和磁扇区对称的双通道能量提取过程，以及极值极限下带电粒子碰撞的高能物理现象。

Method: 基于Lorentz破缺的dyonic Kalb-Ramond黑洞几何，推导电磁和磁荷探测粒子的动力学方程（包括单极感应锥形运动），构造电Penrose和磁Penrose过程（衰变与碰撞机制），分析负能量态、逃逸条件、过度充电/过度磁化可能性，并计算带电粒子碰撞的质心能量及极值极限下的Bañados-Silk-West发散条件。

Result: 电和磁通道分别由Φ_H=Q/[(1-ℓ)r_+]和Ψ_H=p/[(1-2ℓ)r_+]控制；负能量态来源于电磁正则能量而非几何能层；非极端同向碰撞质心能量有限，迎头或近临界构型可产生大能量；极端极限下电或磁临界粒子在满足径向可达条件时产生Bañados-Silk-West发散。

Conclusion: 该Lorentz破缺黑洞为电磁能量提取和粒子加速提供了丰富的物理机制，其电、磁对称的双通道特性与标准RN解有本质区别，极端极限下的临界散射可实现超高能粒子碰撞，具有潜在的天体物理观测意义。

Abstract: This paper is aimed at investigating electromagnetic energy extraction, charged-particle collisions, charged-field superradiance, and horizon stability in a Lorentz-violating dyonic Kalb-Ramond black hole. The geometry differs from the dyonic Reissner-Nordström solution through a modified asymptotic normalization and an effective charge combining electric and magnetic sectors. We derive the dynamics of electrically and magnetically charged probes, including monopole-induced conical motion, and formulate electric and magnetic Penrose processes as decay or collision mechanisms in which a negative-energy fragment falls into the horizon while another escapes with enhanced energy. The electric and magnetic channels are controlled by \(Φ_H=Q/[(1-\ell)r_+]\) and \(Ψ_H=p/[(1-2\ell)r_+]\), respectively. Negative-energy states arise from electromagnetic canonical energy rather than from a geometrical ergoregion. We also obtain escape conditions for the outgoing products, test the possibility of overcharging or overmagnetizing the black hole, and analyze the center-of-mass energy of charged-particle collisions. Nonextremal same-direction collisions remain finite, whereas head-on or near-critical configurations can become large. In the extremal limit, electrically or magnetically critical particles may generate the Bañados-Silk-West divergence when the radial reachability condition is fulfilled.

</details>


### [151] [Emergent vacua and stability constraints on black hole solutions in higher-dimensional $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27297)
*Nicolás Trullols Sandino,Andrei Galiautdinov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究高维f(R)引力中静态球对称真空解，重点关注通过曲率修正项动态生成有效宇宙学常数的可能性。作者证明在五维Starobinsky模型中，纯R²修正无法稳定地生成有效宇宙学常数，其存在必须依赖裸宇宙学常数；对任意维度D和单次项修正f(R)=R+αRⁿ，推导出稳定涌现真空存在的普适条件n>D/2；进一步证明将引力作用量扩展为多项多项式层级可以规避此限制，通过包含至R³阶的曲率修正，可在D≥5维度中实现严格全局无鬼真空时空的动态生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维f(R)引力中能否通过纯几何曲率修正（而非裸宇宙学常数）动态生成稳定的有效宇宙学常数（涌现真空），这对于理解高维引力理论中真空结构、宇宙学常数问题以及修正引力理论的自洽性具有重要意义。此前低维结果未能涵盖高维情形，且单次项修正的稳定性条件尚未系统建立。

Method: 采用分析求解方法：首先在五维Starobinsky模型f(R)=R+αR²-2Λ中对常标量曲率时空施加无鬼稳定性判据f'(R)>0，检验稳定有效宇宙学常数能否由纯R²项生成；然后将分析推广至任意维度D及单次项修正f(R)=R+αRⁿ，推导通用稳定性上界n>D/2；最后引入多项多项式层级作用量（包含至R³阶的修正），利用扩展的几何自由度同时满足迹约束与稳定性判据，并确定确保渐近稳定性和全局稳定性（f'(R)>0对所有R成立）的精确参数空间边界。

Result: (1) 在五维Starobinsky模型中，纯R²几何修正无法稳定生成有效宇宙学常数，必须依赖于裸宇宙学常数Λ；(2) 对一般维度D和单次项修正f(R)=R+αRⁿ，稳定涌现真空存在的普适条件是n>D/2；(3) 当作用量扩展为包含至R³阶修正的多项多项式层级时，该限制可被克服，扩展的几何自由度能同时满足迹约束和稳定性判据；(4) 确定了精确的参数空间边界，保证D≥5维度中严格全局无鬼真空时空的纯几何动态生成。标量子质量条件m_s²>0的完整分析留待未来研究。

Conclusion: 高维f(R)引力中稳定涌现真空的生成能力取决于曲率修正项的阶次结构：单次项修正受限于n>D/2的普适稳定性边界，而多次项多项式作用量可以打破这一限制，在D≥5维中实现严格全局稳定的无鬼真空。这一发现为高维修正引力中规避裸宇宙学常数依赖提供了理论途径，但标量子质量正定性条件尚未完整考虑，需进一步研究。

Abstract: We investigate static spherically symmetric vacuum solutions in higher-dimensional $f(R)$ gravity, beginning with the five-dimensional Starobinsky model governed by the action $f(R) = R + αR^2 - 2Λ$. By enforcing the ghost-free stability criterion $f'(R) > 0$ on constant scalar curvature spacetimes, we show that a stable effective cosmological constant cannot be dynamically generated from pure $R^2$ geometric corrections in five dimensions; its existence is inextricably tied to a bare cosmological constant. Generalizing this analysis to arbitrary dimensions $D$ and single-term curvature corrections $f(R) = R + αR^n$ with a vanishing bare cosmological constant, we derive a universal stability bound, $n > D/2$, required for the existence of stable emergent vacua. Finally, we demonstrate that expanding the gravitational action to a multi-term polynomial hierarchy circumvents this strict limitation. By including curvature corrections up to $\mathcal{O}(R^3)$, the extended geometric degrees of freedom simultaneously satisfy the trace constraint and the stability criterion. Furthermore, we establish the exact parameter space boundaries that ensure not only asymptotic vacuum stability but strict global stability ($f'(R) > 0$ for all $R$), allowing for the dynamical generation of exact, globally ghost-free vacuum spacetimes in $D \ge 5$ purely from higher-order geometric terms. The scalaron mass requirement, $m_s^2>0$ is not imposed in full in our analysis. A detailed investigation of its implications on the models considered in this work are left for future study.

</details>


### [152] [Pair creation in weak gravity and gauge backgrounds: spinning fields and binary stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25540)
*S. A. Franchino-Viñas,N. Varacalli,O. Zanusso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了自旋0、1/2和1的量子场在弱引力和规范背景下的粒子对产生问题，利用重求和的heat-kernel技术，得到了广义曲率二阶封闭形式的对产生概率表达式，并将引力背景下对产生概率用爱因斯坦方程所需的物质分布重新表述，最后以双星系统为例展示了该方法的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子场在引力场和规范场背景下的粒子对产生问题，特别是在弱背景场中理解引力对真空的影响，并希望发展一种能够统一处理不同自旋场的计算方法。

Method: 使用重求和的heat-kernel技术（resummed heat-kernel techniques），推导自旋0、1/2和1的量子场在弱引力场和规范场背景下的对产生概率，将结果表示为广义曲率（generalized curvatures）的二次阶封闭表达式，并通过爱因斯坦方程将引力背景下的结果转化为物质分布的表述。

Result: 获得了自旋0、1/2和1量子场在弱引力与规范背景下的对产生概率封闭表达式（至广义曲率二次阶），并在引力背景下将对产生概率用一致爱因斯坦方程所需的物质分布重新表达。双星系统作为示例验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 重求和heat-kernel方法能够统一且有效地处理不同自旋量子场在弱引力场和规范场背景下的粒子对产生问题，并通过双星系统的应用展示了该方法的实际计算能力和物理意义。

Abstract: We study pair creation for quantum fields of spin $0$, $1/2$, and $1$ in the presence of weak gravitational and gauge backgrounds. Using resummed heat-kernel techniques, we obtain closed expressions for the pair creation probability up to quadratic order in the ``generalized curvatures''. In addition, for gravitational backgrounds, we recast the pair production probability in terms of the matter distribution required for consistency with the Einstein equation. Finally, to illustrate the powerfulness of our methods, we analyze the effect of pair creation in a binary star system.

</details>


### [153] [On the (Non)Unitarity with respect to the Clock of a Dynamical Local Observer and the Einstein Equivalence Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16762)
*Kengo Shimada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究局部观测者时钟定义的内部时间下量子演化的幺正性。在固定弯曲背景上，相对于内部时钟的演化一般是非幺正的，暗示量子层面爱因斯坦等效原理的破坏；而在完全微分同胚不变的动力引力理论中，采用观测者中心坐标后，微分同胚生成元之一在时钟哈密顿量中呈线性，保证相对论演化与剩余约束一致，从而在合理假设有效量子场论存在的前提下恢复幺正演化并保持等效原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力与相对论量子力学中，内部时间（由局部观测者时钟定义）下的量子演化是否保持幺正性，以及微分同胚不变性如何影响这一性质，是理解量子层面等效原理是否成立的关键问题。

Method: 将观测者建模为携带时钟和物质场探测器的相对论粒子，先在固定弯曲背景上分析内部时间演化；随后在完全微分同胚不变的动力引力理论中，利用微分同胚不变性选取观测者中心坐标，在部分约化的相空间上构建与剩余约束一致的相对论演化。

Result: 固定弯曲背景下内部时钟演化一般非幺正，违反量子层面的等效原理；而微分同胚不变引力理论中，通过观测者中心坐标和部分约化，可保证内部时钟下的幺正演化，从而在量子领域保持等效原理。

Conclusion: 微分同胚不变性在塑造局部观测者视角下的关系幺正演化中起根本作用，是维持量子等效原理的必要条件。

Abstract: We investigate the unitarity of quantum evolution relative to an internal time defined by a local observer's clock. The observer is modeled as a relativistic particle carrying both a clock and a matter-field detector, analyzed first on a fixed curved background and subsequently within a fully diffeomorphism-invariant theory of dynamical gravity. In the former case, we find that evolution with respect to the internal clock time is generally nonunitary, implying a violation of the Einstein equivalence principle at the quantum level. In contrast, in the latter case, diffeomorphism invariance allows us to adopt observer-centric coordinates without loss of generality. On the resulting partially-reduced phase space, one of the diffeomorphism generators becomes linear in the clock Hamiltonian, generating a relational evolution that is consistent with the remaining diffeomorphism constraints. Assuming that an effective quantum field theory exists to be consistent with the diffeomorphism invariance, these features ensure unitary evolution relative to the internal clock, thereby preserving the equivalence principle even in the quantum regime. Our results highlight the fundamental role of diffeomorphism invariance in shaping relational unitary evolution from the perspective of a local observer.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>
